We propose two changes to make this algorithm even faster. The first one is a better optimized implementation of the exact same algorithm. The second one is to derive approximate quantiles, using the Cornish-Fisher expansion, instead of exact quantiles. This change removes the necessity to sort the time series, leading to a smaller computational complexity. We call this novel algorithm MomentQuant. We provide evidence that our implementation of Quant is faster than the original one, and that MomentQuant is even faster than our implementation of Quant, at the cost of a tiny decrease in predictive performance. These improvements are especially relevant for real-life applications, where inference is performed much more often than training.

</details>


### [208] [From 80x to 385x: A Best-Matching-Unit Search at the L2 Roof, Measured Against a Symmetrically Tuned Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05138)
*Andrew James Amos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项对称调优的GPU性能比较研究：不仅调优了作者提出的新SOM算法（SparseBin），还同等调优了基线算法（cuSPARSE）。通过四个调优杠杆，SparseBin达到比先前配置快5.6-10.1倍/epoch，相对CUDA实现的优势从~80倍提升到~385倍；cuSPARSE经过对应调优也提速2-3倍。最终调优后的内核达到L2带宽屋顶的77%，其他指标为40-65%，证明这是一个终端结果而非中间站点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出GPU实现之间的比较通常是不对称的：一方算法被作者精心调优，而基线算法往往直接使用现有实现而未加优化。这种不公平比较会歪曲真实的性能优势结论，因此需要一套对称调优的方案来获得可信的比较结果。

Method: 针对自组织映射（SOM）训练中占主导地位的"最佳匹配单元"搜索过程，作者通过四个调优杠杆进行优化：1）tile大小、2）tile成员聚类（tile-membership clustering）、3）神经元轴分块（neuron-axis chunking）、4）向量化加载（vectorised loads）。同时，对基线cuSPARSE算法的每个杠杆对应部分也进行同等调优，以消除比较中的不对称性。

Result: SparseBin在32x32到512x512的地图尺寸范围内，每epoch比先前发布配置快5.6-10.1倍，对CUDA实现的优势从约80倍提升到约385倍。经过对称调优后，cuSPARSE也提速2-3倍。最终调优内核达到L2带宽屋顶的77%（其他单元为40-65%），表明进一步优化空间被限制在约1.3倍以内——这是一个终端结果而非路标，所有未测试的杠杆要么受此上限约束，要么测量为零效果。

Conclusion: 对称调优揭示了真实性能差距：即使基线算法也获得同等级别的优化，SparseBin相对优势依然巨大（从~80x提升至~385x反而说明不对称比较会低估或扭曲性能结论）。此外，通过达到L2带宽屋顶的77%，作者证明了该结果已接近硬件极限，是一个终端结果，并为性能比较方法论提供了重要示范：调优双方而非仅作者一方，才能得出可信、可终局的性能结论。

Abstract: Comparisons between GPU implementations are usually asymmetric: one side is tuned by its author, the other is run as found. I report a programme that tuned both a novel SOM algorithm (SparseBin) and the baseline algorithm it was being compared to (cuSPARSE). The best-matching-unit search that dominates self-organizing map training was tuned through four levers - tile size, tile-membership clustering, neuron-axis chunking and vectorised loads - reaching 5.6-10.1x per epoch over the previously published configuration at map sizes from 32x32 to 512x512, and lifting the margin over the CUDA implementation behind our earlier MEDLINE atlases from ~80x to ~385x. cuSPARSE, the implementation SparseBin is compared against, received every lever with an analogue on its side, and became 2-3x faster in the process. The tuned kernel pressed the L2 bandwidth roof at 77% of peak with every other unit at 40-65%, bounding any further lever at ~1.3x - a terminal result rather than a waypoint, and every untested lever was either capped by that bound by construction or measured null.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Stationarity in Time Series: Discovering Causal Structures and Latent Regimes via Markov Blankets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05150)
*Lei Zan,Charles K. Assaad,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向非平稳时间序列的因果发现算法RCBNB-MB，它能够识别潜在的因果区制（regimes），并在每个区制内发现因果图，通过结合约束与噪声两类因果发现方法并利用马尔可夫毯而非直接父节点，提高了对因果发现错误的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列因果发现方法通常假设存在单一的、时间一致的因果结构，但真实世界动态系统中常存在区制变化（regime changes），这违背了静态因果结构的假设，因此需要一种能处理非平稳时间序列因果结构变化的算法。

Method: RCBNB-MB采用迭代策略：首先将时间序列分割成多个区制（每个区制内因果结构保持稳定），然后在每个区制内发现因果图。该方法结合了约束型（constraint-based）与噪声型（noise-based）因果发现方法，并利用马尔可夫毯（Markov blanket）而非直接父节点，以增强对因果发现误差的鲁棒性并保留预测信息。

Result: 在具有已知真实标签的模拟数据集和真实IT监控数据上的实验表明，RCBNB-MB在准确检测区制变化及其相关因果图方面系统性优于基线方法，并在合理假设下提供了恢复区制转换和因果图的理论保证。

Conclusion: RCBNB-MB是一个鲁棒且通用的非平稳时间序列因果发现框架，能够有效处理区制切换问题，为动态系统中的因果结构推断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Regime-aware Constraint-Based and Noise-Based causal discovery with Markov Blankets (RCBNB-MB), a novel causal discovery algorithm for time series that relaxes the common assumption of a single, time-consistent causal structure. Time series are typically observed at discrete time points and often exhibit regime changes that challenge the assumption of a static causal structure, a limitation in many real-world dynamic systems. To address this challenge, RCBNB-MB identifies latent causal regimes, defined as subsets of time points within which a stable causal structure holds. The algorithm follows an iterative strategy that segments the time series into regimes and discovers the causal graph within each regime. By leveraging the Markov blanket rather than direct parents, RCBNB-MB gains robustness to errors in causal discovery and preserves predictive information. We provide theoretical guarantees for RCBNB-MB's ability to recover both regime transitions and causal graphs under reasonable assumptions. Furthermore, we validate its effectiveness through extensive experiments on simulated datasets with known ground truth and real-world IT monitoring data, where taking into account regime shifts is critical. Empirical results show that RCBNB-MB systematically outperforms baseline approaches in accurately detecting regime changes and their associated causal graphs, positioning it as a robust and versatile framework for non-stationary time series analysis.

</details>


### [210] [Phase Transition Frequency as a Training Time Predictor of Test Accuracy in ResNets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05194)
*Arunan J*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过75个实验（覆盖4个基准和3种ResNet架构），实证检验了ResNet微调期间"离散类别可分性跳跃次数"可作为最终测试准确率的预测指标。在标准i.i.d.分类基准上获得强负相关（CIFAR-10: r=-0.84, CIFAR-100: r=-0.87），但在分布偏移下相关性减弱。作者还通过偏相关分析控制架构深度，并对比了六种替代训练曲线信号，将结果定位为一种分布内训练质量探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种廉价且有效的训练质量预测指标。现有的最强竞争者（如有效秩、Hessian锐度、Fisher信息、边际和神经坍缩度量等）均未被本研究纳入比较，作者希望探索训练曲线中类别可分性跳跃次数这一简单信号，作为最终测试准确率的早期预测器。

Method: 在75个实验配置中（3种ResNet架构×多基准×5-10个种子），经验性地计算ResNet微调过程中离散类别可分性跳跃的次数，将其与最终测试准确率做相关性分析。采用皮尔逊相关系数评估线性关系，使用偏相关控制架构深度协变量，并与六种替代训练曲线信号进行比较。提供适合标准训练循环旁记录的低成本检测程序。

Result: 标准i.i.d.基准上得到强负相关：CIFAR-10上r=-0.84（p<10^-8, n=30），CIFAR-100上r=-0.87（p<10^-5, n=15）。分布偏移下关系减弱：TinyImageNet r=-0.45，CIFAR-10-C r=-0.19。控制架构深度后，CIFAR-100的偏相关仍显著（r_partial=-0.69, p=0.007）。与六种替代信号相比，跳跃次数在CIFAR-100上最强、CIFAR-10上接近最强，但在两个压力基准上被其他信号超越。

Conclusion: 类别可分性跳跃次数可作为有效的分布内训练质量探针，尤其是在标准i.i.d.分类任务中。该指标在分布偏移场景下的预测能力有限，需谨慎使用。未来工作应将其与文献中最强的竞争者（有效秩、Hessian锐度、Fisher信息等指标）进行系统比较，进一步定位其适用边界。

Abstract: The number of discrete class-separability jumps observed during ResNet finetuning is examined empirically as a predictor of final test accuracy. Across 75 experiments spanning four benchmarks (CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, TinyImageNet, and CIFAR-10-C) and three architectures (ResNet-18, ResNet-50, and ResNet-101), with five to ten seeds per configuration, a strong within-dataset negative correlation is obtained on standard i.i.d. classification benchmarks: \(r = -0.84\) on CIFAR-10 (\(p < 10^{-8}\), \(n = 30\)) and \(r = -0.87\) on CIFAR-100 (\(p < 10^{-5}\), \(n = 15\)). Under distributional stress, the relationship attenuates: TinyImageNet yields \(r = -0.45\), and the CIFAR-10-C corruption benchmark yields \(r = -0.19\). Two additional analyses discipline the empirical claim. A partial correlation controlling for architecture depth, treated as a linear covariate, shows that on CIFAR-100 the transition count retains statistically significant predictive power (\(r_{\mathrm{partial}} = -0.69\), \(p = 0.007\)); the corresponding result under the stricter categorical conditioning is not established at \(n = 15\). A comparison against six alternative training-curve signals shows that transition count achieved the strongest correlation among the evaluated signals on CIFAR-100 and one of the strongest on CIFAR-10, but is dominated by other signals on the two stressed benchmarks. The comparison is restricted to training-curve-level signals; comparisons against effective rank, Hessian sharpness, Fisher information, margin, and neural-collapse measures, which are the strongest competitors in the current literature, are not part of the present study and remain open. The observation is presented as an in-distribution training-quality probe among a family of candidate probes, and an inexpensive detection procedure suitable for logging alongside a standard training loop is provided.

</details>


### [211] [Dimension-Adaptive Batched Lipschitz Narrowing Without Knowing the Zooming Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05214)
*Yasong Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Count-Adaptive BLiN算法，消除了ACE序列对缩放维度d_z和缩放常数C_z的依赖，通过基于前一轮淘汰后存活立方体数量来选择下一轮边长，实现了与原有算法相同的遗憾界和最优批次复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始的A-BLiN算法的ACE序列依赖于缩放维度d_z，这在实际问题中往往是未知的。需要一种不依赖d_z和C_z的算法，同时保持原有的理论保证。

Method: 新的边长选择机制不再显式使用d_z或C_z，而是统计前一轮淘汰后存活的立方体数量，并从这个计数中自适应地确定下一轮的边长（即Count-Adaptive策略）。

Result: 该算法在不使用d_z和C_z的情况下，仍达到了Õ_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})的遗憾界，批次复杂度为O_d(log log T)。结合原文定理10的自适应网格下界，当d_z未知时，最优批次复杂度仍为Θ_d(log log T)。

Conclusion: Count-Adaptive BLiN在无需知道缩放维度的情况下仍能保持最优的批次复杂度，这标志着对原始BLiN框架的一个重要理论改进，使其更具实用性。

Abstract: The Appropriately Combined Edge-length (ACE) sequence in A-BLiN depends on the zooming dimension $d_z$. This note removes that dependence. The next edge length is selected from the number of cubes that survive the preceding elimination. The resulting Count-Adaptive BLiN algorithm does not use $d_z$ or the zooming constant $C_z$, yet it attains $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_d(T^{(d_z+1)/(d_z+2)})$ regret with $\mathcal O_d(\log\log T)$ batches. Together with the adaptive-grid lower bound in Theorem 10 of the original paper, the optimal batch complexity remains $Θ_d(\log\log T)$ when $d_z$ is unknown.

</details>


### [212] [FedDRAW: Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting for Heterogeneous Multi-Institutional Chest Radiograph Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05223)
*Maryam Moradpour,Anne-Christin Hauschild*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDRAW（联邦双声誉退火加权法），一种服务器端聚合方法，用于联邦学习中跨医院训练医学诊断模型。它结合数据量先验与客户端/全局参数余弦相似度，通过内外两个耦合的退火调度来控制加权，解决FedAvg中大数据量客户端主导全局模型而忽略小型但信息丰富医院的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习允许医院在不共享患者隐私数据的情况下联合训练诊断模型，但传统FedAvg按样本数量比例分配聚合权重，导致小型但信息丰富的医院影响力被永久低估，而大型客户可能即使信息量较低也主导全局模型，造成模型偏差。

Method: FedDRAW是一种服务器端聚合方法，将数据规模先验与客户端和全局参数之间的余弦相似度相结合，并采用两个耦合的退火调度：内部调度使客户端声誉从规模先验转向相似度；外部延迟的softmax逆温度退火调度在早期和中轮次保持权重选择性，并在收敛时放宽至均匀分布。

Result: 在12个模拟客户端分区的胸部X光数据集（CheXpert和ChestMNIST）场景中，FedDRAW在AUC和敏感性/特异性几何均值（GM）方面均获得八种方法中的最高平均排名，Friedman检验与Nemenyi事后分析确认方法间差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 调度两个信号而非仅按样本数固定权重，能够实现更少偏差的诊断模型。这意味着综合考虑数据量与信息质量的双重信号调度策略，有望促进更公平、更准确的联邦医学诊断。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence models are promising for medical diagnosis, but they require large numbers of unbiased data, which in medicine are distributed across hospitals and cannot be centralized to protect patient privacy. Federated Learning (FL) addresses this, since hospitals train one shared diagnostic model while patient data remain local. Training proceeds in communication rounds, in which each hospital trains the shared model locally and returns it to the server for merging by weighted average. This aggregation weight determines whose institutional knowledge shapes the result. Federated averaging (FedAvg) sets it in proportion to local sample count, so a small but informative hospital is permanently assigned a small influence, andl argest clients could dominate the global model even when they are less informative. We propose Federated Dual Reputation Annealing Weighting (FedDRAW), a server-side aggregation method that combines a data-size prior with the cosine similarity between client and global parameters under two coupled annealing schedules. An inner schedule shifts client reputation from the size prior towards similarity. An outer, deferred annealing schedule on the softmax inverse temperature keeps the weighting selective in the early and middle rounds and relaxes it to uniformity at convergence. We evaluate FedDRAW on 12 simulated client-partition scenarios of two chest radiograph datasets (CheXpert and ChestMNIST), against seven federated baselines under identical local training settings. FedDRAW achieved the highest average rank among all eight methods under both AUC and the geometric mean (GM) of sensitivity and specificity, which a Friedman test with Nemenyi post-hoc analysis confirmed to be a statistically significant difference between the methods. Scheduling two signals, rather than fixing the weights by sample count alone, could enable less biased diagnostic models.

</details>


### [213] [Hessian-based molecular conformation augmentation for a scalable and efficient strategy of machine learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05233)
*Bumju Kwak,Jeonghee Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于Hessian的数据增强方案（UniAug和ModeAug），通过简单的泰勒展开生成位移样本，在不修改训练目标或扩展autograd图的前提下，将Hessian信息融入机器学习原子间势（MLIP）训练，从而提升模型精度并支持振动分析与过渡态搜索等应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLIP通常仅基于能量和原子力训练，忽略了Hessian信息；而现有的显式引入Hessian的方法需要修改网络架构，且因高阶反向传播带来巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 提出两种数据增强方案：UniAug（各向同性高斯位移）和ModeAug（简正模式加权位移）。两者均利用简单泰勒展开生成增强样本，不改变训练目标，也不扩展autograd计算图，可实现即插即用集成。

Result: 在非平衡和平衡数据集上的综合评估表明，该方法提升了模型精度，并提供了面向具体任务（task-specific）的实用指导准则。

Conclusion: 通过低成本的数据增强策略有效利用Hessian信息，兼顾精度提升与计算效率，为MLIP在振动分析和过渡态搜索等实际应用中的性能提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: While machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) have successfully learned potential energy surfaces (PES) and atomic forces, many practical applications, such as vibrational analysis and transition state search, rely heavily on the PES Hessian. Yet, standard MLIPs tend to be trained on energy and forces alone, leaving Hessian information largely unexploited. Meanwhile, existing methods that explicitly incorporate the Hessian into training objectives require architectural modifications and introduce significant computational and memory overheads due to higher-order backpropagation. To address these limitations, we propose two Hessian-derived data augmentation schemes: isotropic Gaussian displacement (\textbf{UniAug}) and normal mode-weighted displacement (\textbf{ModeAug}). Both methods utilize simple Taylor expansions, achieving effective augmentation without altering training objectives or extending the autograd graph. This allows seamless, plug-and-play integration with existing architectures and training pipelines. Comprehensive evaluations across non-equilibrium and equilibrium datasets demonstrate that our approach enhances model accuracy while providing practical, task-specific guidelines.

</details>


### [214] [PRICE: A Systematic Study of LLM Adaptation Choices for Bitcoin Price Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05235)
*Maryam Fakhari,Mehran Safayani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRICE方法，将LLM适配于短期比特币价格预测，结合LoRA微调、递归多步推理、整数取整数值表示、CTF提示和零温度解码等组件，在8个基准模型的对比中取得最低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场具有极端波动性和非平稳动态特征，传统预测方法面临挑战。虽然LLM在时间序列预测中展现出潜力，但在金融场景下，各种适配选择（如微调、数值表示、提示、推理和解码方式）的综合影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: PRICE基于4-bit量化的LLaMA-3 8B模型，系统性地整合了五个关键组件：1) 基于LoRA的参数高效微调；2) 递归多步推理；3) 整数取整的数值表示；4) Context-Task-Format (CTF)提示策略；5) 精确零温度解码。并通过消融实验逐一验证各组件贡献。

Result: 消融实验表明每个组件均对预测精度和可靠性有贡献：LoRA支持在有限硬件上高效训练，递归推理提升精度，整数取整降低误差，CTF提示优于CoT、iCoT和few-shot提示，零温度解码提升递归预测稳定性。与8个基于Transformer和时间序列基础模型的对比中，PRICE在验证集和测试集上均取得最低预测误差。

Conclusion: 尽管PRICE基于以文本而非时间序列数据预训练为主的模型，仍能达到或超越专门基础模型的性能。研究表明适配选择对LLM在数值时间序列预测中的准确性和鲁棒性起决定性作用。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets exhibit extreme volatility and non-stationary dynamics that challenge conventional forecasting methods. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise for time series forecasting, the combined effects of adaptation choices remain largely unexplored in financial settings. This study introduces PRICE, a structured approach for adapting LLMs to short-term Bitcoin price forecasting. Built on a 4-bit quantized LLaMA-3 8B model, PRICE investigates how fine-tuning, numerical representation, prompting, inference, and decoding jointly influence forecasting performance. PRICE integrates Parameter-efficient fine-tuning with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), Recursive multi-step inference, Integer-rounded numerical representation, Context-Task-Format (CTF) prompting, and Exact zero-temperature decoding. Ablation studies show that each component contributes to forecasting accuracy and reliability. LoRA enables efficient training on limited hardware, recursive inference improves accuracy, integer-rounded values reduce errors, CTF prompting outperforms Chain-of-Thought, Implicit Chain-of-Thought (iCoT), and few-shot prompting, and zero-temperature decoding improves stability during recursive forecasting. Comparative evaluation against eight transformer-based and time-series foundation models shows that PRICE achieves the lowest forecasting errors on both validation and test sets while maintaining robust performance across evaluation periods. Despite being based on a model primarily pretrained on text rather than time-series data, PRICE achieves competitive or superior performance relative to specialized foundation models. These findings demonstrate that adaptation choices critically determine the accuracy and robustness of LLMs for numerical time-series forecasting.

</details>


### [215] [GLASS: Graph-Language Alignment with Spherical Scoring for Transferable Graph-Level Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05253)
*Xudong Wang,Chris Ding,Tongxin Li,Jicong Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS是一个面向图级异常检测的框架，通过图-语言对齐在单位超球面上构建统一表征空间，利用Graph Descriptor Prompt（GraphDP）作为文本桥接实现跨领域迁移。它采用多切片软余弦目标和Matryoshka表征切片保证多尺度一致性，并提出球形多模态评分（SMS）方法，利用von Mises-Fisher核密度估计在图和文本嵌入空间中实现异常检测，支持零样本和少样本跨域检测。在12个基准和3个元领域上取得了最优平均AUROC及排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图级异常检测方法通常局限于单一领域或需要目标域训练数据，缺乏跨域可迁移性。作者旨在通过图-语言对齐建立统一的表征空间，使模型无需目标域数据（或仅需少量正常样本）即可在不同领域间实现有效的异常检测。

Method: GLASS包含三大核心组件：(1) 将结构感知图编码器与指令感知文本嵌入通过多切片软余弦目标在单位超球面上对齐；(2) 将局部、全局和语义图属性序列化为紧凑的Graph Descriptor Prompt（GraphDP），作为领域无关异常评分的文本桥；(3) 通过Matryoshka表征切片实现多尺度一致性；(4) 提出球形多模态评分（SMS），在图和文本嵌入空间分别实例化von Mises-Fisher核密度估计，实现基于密度估计的异常评分，可恢复角度k近邻评分为高浓度极限情况。

Result: 在12个基准数据集和3个元领域上的实验表明，与近年先进的GLAD基线相比，GLASS取得了最优的平均AUROC和排名；通过编码目标域的GraphDP而不使用目标域训练数据即可实现零样本异常检测，仅需少量正常样本即可通过参考集校准完成少样本适应。

Conclusion: GLASS通过图-语言对齐和GraphDP文本桥接成功实现了图级异常检测的鲁棒跨域迁移，其概率化评分框架统一了结构与语义异常信号，为零样本和少样本设定下的领域自适应GLAD提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce GLASS, a framework for graph-level anomaly detection (GLAD) that achieves robust cross-domain transferability through graph-language alignment on the unit hypersphere. GLASS builds a unified representation space by aligning a structure-aware graph encoder with an instruction-aware text embedding via a multi-slice soft cosine objective. Our framework serializes local, global, and semantic graph properties into a compact Graph Descriptor Prompt (GraphDP), creating a text bridge that enables domain-agnostic anomaly scoring. By enforcing multi-scale consistency through Matryoshka representation slices, the model captures anomalous deviations at multiple levels of granularity. For scoring, we formulate anomaly detection as density estimation on the aligned hypersphere and introduce Spherical Multi-Modal Scoring (SMS), which instantiates von Mises-Fisher kernel density estimators in both graph and text embedding spaces. This probabilistic formulation recovers angular k-nearest-neighbor scoring as a high-concentration limiting case and provides a principled fusion of structural and semantic anomaly signals. The shared text embedding space further serves as a cross-domain bridge: by encoding a target domain's GraphDP without target-domain training data, GLASS performs zero-shot anomaly detection, and with only a handful of normal examples, few-shot adaptation via reference-set calibration. Across twelve benchmarks and three meta-domains, GLASS obtains the best average AUROC and rank compared with recent advanced GLAD baselines and enables effective cross-domain transfer.

</details>


### [216] [How to Speculate about Uncertainty in Agentic Coding? A Draft-Model Gate Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05274)
*Konstantin Grotov,Valentin Malykh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"投机不确定性"(Speculative Uncertainty)的方法，仅通过黑盒LLM智能体输出token来预测其失败信号，无需访问logits、权重、激活或重复采样。该方法利用小型开源草稿模型对已生成的轨迹进行评分，提取阶段感知特征并校准，生成可被下游策略直接消费的失败似然分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程领域的LLM智能体失败代价高昂：它们经常自信地犯错，而糟糕的行动只有在昂贵的执行和重试之后才被识别。现有方法要么需要访问模型内部信息（logits、权重、激活），要么需要重复采样，这在黑盒场景下不可行。需要一种仅从输出token就能获得预测性失败信号的方法。

Method: 1) 反转投机解码的思想：使用小型开放权重草稿模型对智能体已经生成的轨迹进行单次前向打分；2) 从这些投机性交叉似然中提取特征，通过分离推理(action reasoning)和行动(action)片段获得阶段感知特征；3) 针对可验证的目标校准这些特征；4) 输出一个失败似然分数，供路由、人工干预或额外测试时计算等下游策略直接使用。

Result: 在软件工程智能体Qwen3-Coder-480B和闭源Claude 3.5 Sonnet上部署"执行前否决门"(pre-execution veto gate)策略，执行错误率降低6-8个百分点，token成本降低14-19%；可无需重新训练地迁移到分布外基准，并能跨智能体模型泛化。

Conclusion: 投机不确定性方法证明：即使没有模型内部信息，仅从黑盒智能体的输出token中恢复预测性失败信号是可行的，且该信号具有实际可操作性，能够显著降低执行错误和token成本，同时具备良好的跨分布和跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents deployed for software engineering fail expensively: they act confidently wrong, and bad actions are recognized only after costly execution and retry. We present Speculative Uncertainty (SU), a method that recovers a predictive failure signal for a black-box agent from its output tokens alone, with no access to logits, weights, activations, or repeated sampling. Inverting speculative decoding, a small open-weight draft model scores the agent's already-generated trajectory in a single forward pass. From these speculative cross-likelihoods we extract phase-aware features by separating the reasoning and action spans, and calibrate them against a verifiable objective. SU produces a failure-likelihood score that any downstream policy, such as routing, human intervention, or extra test-time compute, can consume directly. To show the signal is actionable, we instantiate one such policy, a pre-execution veto gate, on software engineering agents Qwen3-Coder-480B and closed-source Claude 3.5 Sonnet, cutting execution error rate by 6-8 percentage points and token cost by 14-19% in deployment, transferring to out-of-distribution benchmarks without retraining, and generalizing across agent models.

</details>


### [217] [Learning from VAE Errors to support ECG-based Differential Diagnosis of Myocardial Scar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05294)
*Shayan Sharifi,Riccardo Treu,Ilaria Gandin,Federico Garoia,Marco Merlo,Giulia Cisotto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了两种基于ECG的表示学习方法（基础模型ECGx.AI和浅层β-VAE）能否通过心电图判别心肌病患者的晚期钆增强（LGE）状态。ECGx.AI达到AUC 0.686，而β-VAE虽单独分类性能较低（AUC 0.577），但其基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重构误差在12导联中的10个导联上显示出显著的类间差异，且可作为有效的分类特征（AUC 0.643），提示DTW重构误差有潜力作为瘢痕相关ECG改变的生物标志物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏磁共振LGE是心肌瘢痕的关键标志物，但其可及性有限，因此需要基于常规ECG的替代性筛查方法。作者希望通过无监督表征学习方法来判别LGE+与LGE-的心肌病患者，以弥补心脏磁共振资源短缺的不足。

Method: 在300名心肌病患者的本地队列中，比较了两种方法提取的32维ECG特征：(1)基于基础模型ECGx.AI的特征；(2)在正常PTB-XL ECGs上训练的浅层β-VAE特征。评估方案包括下游分类任务（随机森林、梯度提升、逻辑回归）以及基于动态时间规整（DTW）的重构误差分析。

Result: ECGx.AI配合随机森林达到AUC 0.686；β-VAE配合梯度提升仅达AUC 0.577但灵敏度0.775；DTW重构误差在12导联中的10个导联上表现出显著的类间差异（Mann-Whitney U检验），配合逻辑回归达到AUC 0.643。

Conclusion: DTW重构误差在区分LGE+与LGE-患者方面具有统计学显著性和实际分类价值，有望成为心肌瘢痕相关ECG改变的潜在生物标志物。尽管基础模型性能更优，但基于重构误差的方法提供了可解释性强且无需大量标注数据的新途径。

Abstract: Late Gadolinium Enhancement (LGE) on cardiac magnetic resonance is a key marker of myocardial scar, but its limited accessibility motivates routine ECG-based screening. We evaluated whether $β$-variational autoencoder (VAE)-derived ECG representations can discriminate LGE+ from LGE- cardiomyopathic patients in a local cohort of 300 subjects. We compared 32-dimensional features from the foundation ECGx.AI model with those from a shallower $β$-VAE trained on normal PTB-XL ECGs, evaluating downstream classification and Dynamic Time Warping (DTW)-based reconstruction errors. ECGx.AI reached an area under ROC of 0.686 with Random Forest, while the proposed $β$-VAE reached 0.577 with sensitivity of 0.775 with Gradient Boosting. Notably, DTW-reconstruction errors significantly differed between classes in 10 out of 12 leads according to Mann-Whitney U test and help in classification, leading to an area under ROC of 0.643 with Logistic Regression, supporting their potential as markers of scar-related ECG alterations.

</details>


### [218] [How Does mHC Use Its Residual Streams? Selective Routing and Near-Identity Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05309)
*Pengxiang Zhao,Xing Li,Xianzhi Yu,Wei Guo,Zhenhua Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了DeepSeek-V4-Flash中四流残差通路（基于Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC）的实际使用方式。通过对有效流数量、跨流残差权重和流间余弦相似度的分析，发现读写路由集中但随深度变化，典型的attention或FFN站点实际约使用两个流；残差混合主要发生在早期层，后期层（22-42）各流基本独立前向传播。定向干预实验表明，晚期混合器替换为恒等映射仅使困惑度增加1.9%并保持任务平均分，而早期混合器替换则使困惑度增加41%。早期混合器固定为诊断均值仅增加0.2%困惑度且评分仅降0.25个百分点，保留每token最大三个路由权重最多增加2.7%困惑度。结论是模型仅实现了四流mHC所提供灵活性的一部分：单个块很少需要全部四个流，晚期残差混合几乎没有可测量的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hyper-Connections及其流形约束变体mHC将残差通路从单一流扩展为n个流，扩大了模型的表达能力。然而，训练后的模型如何利用这种容量仍不清楚——包括块读取和写入的广度、残差通路混合各流的强度，以及各流是否携带不同的表征。理解这些使用模式有助于揭示架构设计的有效性并为后续优化提供依据。

Method: 使用三种量化工具分析DeepSeek-V4-Flash的四流残差通路：(1)有效流数量来衡量块读取/写入的广度；(2)跨流残差权重来衡量流间混合强度；(3)流间余弦相似度来衡量各流表征的方向性差异。随后设计了定向干预实验：将晚期混合器替换为恒等映射、替换早期混合器、将早期混合器固定为其C4诊断均值、以及仅保留每token最大的三个路由权重，评估其对C4困惑度和六任务平均得分的影响。

Result: 读写路由集中但随深度变化：典型的attention或FFN站点有效使用约两个流，主导流随层变化，表征保持方向性差异。残差混合幅度小且主要出现在早期层；在层22-42之间，通路基本独立前向传递各流。替换晚期混合器为恒等映射仅使C4困惑度增加1.9%并保持六任务平均得分；替换早期混合器则使困惑度增加41%。将每个早期混合器固定为其C4诊断均值仅使困惑度增加0.2%，平均得分降低0.25个百分点。保留每站点每token的三个最大路由权重时，困惑度最多增加2.7%，平均得分最多变化0.4点。

Conclusion: DeepSeek-V4-Flash仅实现了四流mHC所提供灵活性的一部分：单个块很少需要全部四个流，且晚期残差混合在评估指标上几乎没有带来可测量的收益。早期层的混合结构具有重要的功能意义——其站点特异性结构比逐token变化更为关键，而晚期混合器实际上可以被移除而几乎不影响性能，这暗示了当前架构存在进一步简化的空间。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections and their manifold-constrained variant mHC widen a residual pathway from one stream to n, yet how trained models use this capacity remains unclear: how broadly blocks read and write, how strongly the residual pathway mixes streams, and whether the streams carry distinct representations. We examine these properties in the four-stream residual pathway of DeepSeek-V4-Flash using effective stream counts, cross-stream residual weights, and inter-stream cosine similarity. Read/write routing is concentrated but varies across depth: a typical attention or FFN site effectively uses about two streams, while the dominant stream changes across layers and the representations remain directionally distinct. Residual mixing is modest and occurs primarily in early layers; in layers 22-42, the pathway mostly carries each stream forward separately. Targeted interventions establish the functional significance of these patterns. Replacing the late mixers by identity increases C4 perplexity by only 1.9% and preserves the six-task average score, whereas replacing the early mixers increases perplexity by 41%. Fixing each early mixer to its C4 diagnostic mean increases perplexity by only 0.2% and reduces the average score by 0.25 percentage points, showing that its site-specific structure matters more than its token-wise variation on the evaluated metrics. Likewise, retaining the three largest routing weights per token at every site increases perplexity by at most 2.7% and changes the average score by at most 0.4 points. Thus, the studied model realizes only part of the flexibility afforded by four-stream mHC: individual blocks rarely require all four streams, and late residual mixing provides little measured benefit.

</details>


### [219] [Optimal Rates for Agentic Networked Information Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05318)
*MohammadHossein Bateni,Zahra Hadizadeh,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi,Mahdi JafariRaviz,Shayan Taherijam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究网络化学习模型中的信息聚合问题，收紧了Kearns等人关于线性回归中多余均方误差率的上下界差距：正确速率在深度M²之前是常数，之后是Θ(M²/D)。作者还证明了逻辑回归（logistic classification）具有相同的最优速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kearns, Roth和Ryu提出了一个网络化学习模型（对应agentic AI模式：每个agent只看到部分数据和其父节点的结论），并证明了深度为D的M覆盖路径上最后一个agent的额外MSE上界为O(M/√D)，同时给出下界Ω(M/D)，两者之间存在明显的gap。本工作的动机是彻底关闭这一gap，确定准确的最优速率。

Method: 1) 对循环实例进行更精细的分析，将下界改进为D<M²时Ω(√(M/D))；2) 对每个深度D≥M²构造M覆盖路径，证明额外误差下界为Ω(M²/D)；3) 证明对任何固定分布，额外误差沿路径呈几何收缩，排除单一实例在所有深度同时达到多项式下界的可能性；4) 将上述结果推广到logistic分类（BCE损失）的logit-passing模型中，通过证明logistic路径在这些例子中与最小二乘路径一致（仅差缩放）来转移所有下界。

Result: 成功关闭gap：证明了准确率在深度达到M²之前是常数级别的额外误差，之后为Θ(M²/D)。同时证明了同样的最优速率适用于logistic分类下的logit-passing模型。

Conclusion: 本文给出了网络化信息聚合在MSE回归和BCE分类下的精确多余误差速率，即深度M²之前的常数界和之后的M²/D速率，该结论同时统一了回归与分类两种设定。

Abstract: Building on the pioneering paper of Kearns, Roth, and Ryu (SODA'26), we study information aggregation in a networked learning model. The model captures a central pattern in agentic AI: each agent sees only part of the data and passes on only its own conclusion. Their model considers a linear regression problem with the mean squared error (MSE) loss. Agents sit in a DAG and each sees only a subset of the features and its parents' predictions, fits a linear predictor, and passes only its prediction forward. The benchmark is the full-feature learner that sees all raw features. A path of depth $D$ is $M$-covered if every block of $M$ consecutive agents collectively sees all raw features. Kearns, Roth, and Ryu proved that the excess mean squared error of the last agent on such a path is $O(M/\sqrt D)$, and gave a cyclic instance with excess error $Ω(M/D)$ for $D<M^2$.
  We close this gap: the correct rate is constant up to depth $M^2$, and $Θ(M^2/D)$ beyond it. We first give a sharper analysis of the cyclic instance and improve its lower bound to $Ω(\sqrt{M/D})$ for $D<M^2$. We then construct, for every depth $D\ge M^2$, an $M$-covered path of depth $D$ with excess error $Ω(M^2/D)$. The same instance gives the constant lower bound for all $D < M^2$. We also show that for any fixed distribution the excess error contracts geometrically along the path, ruling out any single instance that witnesses any polynomial lower bound at every depth.
  Finally, we prove the same optimal rate for logistic classification in the logit-passing model of Bateni et al., which considers the binary cross-entropy (BCE) loss. The same improved upper bound of $O(M^2/D)$ holds, and we transfer all the regression lower bounds by showing that on those examples the logistic path follows the least-squares path up to rescaling.

</details>


### [220] [Embedded Graph Flows for Categorical Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05328)
*Ethan Ma,Zihan Wang,Chris Siu Yeung Chow,Xinguo Feng,Qingqing Li,Rui Jiang,Naipeng Dong,Guangdong Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EGF（嵌入式图流）是一种生成式图模型，为节点和边类别学习连续嵌入，并用置换等变图变换器把高斯噪声传输到这些学习到的终点，最后通过读出层把嵌入映射回离散类别。在QM9上四项指标全部最优（FCD低至0.150），在ZINC250k上NSPDK-MMD也最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图生成器将类别编码为固定one-hot向量，导致类别在几何上等距分布，这是一种人为且不自然的几何限制，无法捕捉类别间的真实关系或结构语义。

Method: 1) 为节点和边类别学习连续嵌入(而非固定one-hot)；2) 利用置换等变图变换器，将高斯噪声传输(flow)到学习到的类别嵌入端点；3) 通过末端读出层将嵌入映射回离散图类别。

Result: 在QM9基准上，EGF四项指标全部为最优，FCD=0.150，远优于DiGress(0.717)和GruM(0.812)。在ZINC250k更大分子上，EGF的NSPDK-MMD最低，说明其生成的局部子结构最接近参考分子。

Conclusion: 学习连续类别嵌入替代固定one-hot编码是可行且有效的思路，能在分子图生成任务上显著超越基于类别扩散的基线方法，且在大分子上依然保持优异的局部结构保真度。

Abstract: Generating categorical graphs requires choosing node and edge types that form a coherent structure without depending on node order. Many graph generators encode categories as fixed one-hot vectors, which can impose an artificial geometry in which categories are equidistant. We propose Embedded Graph Flows (EGF), a generative model that learns continuous embeddings for node and unordered-edge categories and transports Gaussian noise towards these learnt endpoints using a permutation-equivariant graph transformer. A terminal readout maps the embeddings back to discrete graph categories. Across molecular benchmarks, EGF achieved competitive performance. On QM9, EGF gives the best result on all four reported metrics among the three methods, including a Fréchet ChemNet Distance (FCD) of 0.150, compared with 0.717 for the categorical-diffusion baseline DiGress and 0.812 for the bridge-based baseline GruM. When applied to larger molecules in ZINC250k, EGF retains the lowest maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) using the neighbourhood subgraph pairwise distance kernel (NSPDK), indicating close agreement with the local substructures of the reference molecules. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trusted-System-Lab/EGF.

</details>


### [221] [Variational Continuation for Double Pendulum Periodic Orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05337)
*Leo Yao,Ziming Liu,Max Tegmark*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Hessian的方法来数值延拓动力系统中的周期轨道。该方法将周期轨道参数化为傅里叶级数，利用自动微分（而非手工推导的Jacobian）定义并优化损失函数，通过损失景观的平坦方向（零特征值方向）确定延拓方向，实现无积分器、可精确初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡，并能高效检测轨道族的相交与次谐波分岔。作者以双摆周期振荡的完整延拓作为演示，发现了文献中缺失的两摆质量从不同时静止的周期轨道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统周期轨道延拓方法依赖于手工推导的Jacobian矩阵，过程繁琐且易出错；现有方法往往需要积分器，且难以精确初始化不稳定不动点附近的振荡。作者希望利用机器学习中的自动微分技术自动化这一过程，并通过Hessian的平坦方向来引导延拓，使搜索更高效和系统化。

Method: 将周期轨道候选（闭环）参数化为傅里叶级数，构造度量循环偏离物理微分方程的损失函数；利用自动微分自动计算梯度与Hessian，无需手工推导Jacobian；通过Hessian的零特征值方向（平坦方向）确定延拓方向，引导周期轨道的搜索与延续；该方法不依赖积分器。

Result: 在双摆系统中完成了从不动点出发的完整周期振荡延拓，展示了沿轨道族的分岔现象并对周期轨道分支进行了分类；特别发现了两个摆质量从不同时处于静止状态的周期轨道，该类型轨道据作者所知在已有文献中缺失。

Conclusion: 基于Hessian和自动微分的方法为周期轨道延拓提供了一种通用、自动化的新范式，能高效检测轨道族相交与次谐波分岔，并可发现新的物理现象（如双摆的特殊周期轨道），展示了该方法在非线性动力学研究中的潜力。

Abstract: We present a Hessian-based approach to numerically continue periodic orbits in dynamical systems. A loop (periodic orbit candidate) is parametrized as a Fourier series; a loss function is defined based on the deviation of the loop from the physical differential equations. Unlike previous work relying on hand-derived Jacobians, our method automates the process by leveraging automatic differentiation, a common machine learning technique. The continuation direction can be determined by the flat directions of the loss landscapes (directions with zero eigenvalues), making the search of periodic orbits efficient and guided. Our method is integrator-free, precisely initializes oscillations around unstable fixed points, and efficiently detects orbit family intersections and subharmonic bifurcations. As a demonstration, we present full continuations of periodic double pendulum oscillations from fixed points, showing bifurcations along orbit families and categorizing branches of periodic orbits. In particular, we find periodic orbits where both pendulum masses are never simultaneously at rest, which to our knowledge has been missing in the literature.

</details>


### [222] [Distill Globally, Adapt Locally: Reasoning Distillation and Product-Type Test-Time Training for Scalable Trade-Up Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05363)
*Siliang Liu,Mohammad Ghasemi,Sapan Patel,Amin Banitalebi-Dehkordi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两级框架，将LLM推理蒸馏为高效的非生成式学生模型，用于升级推荐（trade-up recommendation）。Level 1通过检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化标签和自然语言理由，监督紧凑的嵌入对分类器；Level 2通过产品类型特定的测试时训练（PT-TTT）微调轻量适配器。学生模型仅需两个预计算的768维商品嵌入，无需LLM调用。在8,352对人工标注基准上AUC达0.924，PT-TTT提升至0.941；相比直接LLM推理快约5,000倍、成本低10,000倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 升级推荐需要识别保留购买意图同时提供更高品质的替代品。LLM虽能推理这类区分，但直接应用于数亿商品对在操作上不可行，需要兼顾推理质量与规模化效率。

Method: 两级框架：(1) Level 1：检索增强的少样本LLM教师生成结构化关系标签和自然语言理由，通过对齐和对比目标蒸馏到紧凑的嵌入对分类器（15.5M参数四分类学生），推理时仅用两个预计算768维嵌入；(2) Level 2：产品类型测试时训练（PT-TTT），利用少样本示范为冻结的学生模型优化轻量类别特定适配器。

Result: 8,352对人工标注基准上，推理蒸馏学生AUC 0.924，优于仅标签学生0.912；PT-TTT将AUC从0.924提升至0.941，平均精度从0.920提升至0.940；在100K对代理目录上，单台八GPU机器上相比直接LLM推理快约5,000倍、估计成本低10,000倍。

Conclusion: 该两级框架成功地将LLM的推理能力蒸馏到高效的非生成式模型中，同时通过产品类型特定的测试时训练实现决策边界的自适应，在保持高准确性的同时实现了巨大的计算效率和成本优势，为大规模升级推荐提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Trade-up recommendation identifies higher-quality alternatives that preserve a customer's purchase intent while offering upgraded benefits. Large language models (LLMs) can reason about such distinctions, but applying them directly to hundreds of millions of product pairs is operationally impractical. We introduce a two-level framework that distills LLM reasoning into an efficient non-generative student and adapts its decision boundary to product-type-specific trade-up criteria. At Level 1, a retrieval-augmented few-shot LLM teacher generates structured relation labels and natural-language rationales. These rationales supervise a compact embedding-pair classifier through alignment and contrastive objectives; at inference, the student uses only two precomputed 768-dimensional product embeddings, with no LLM calls or text generation. On a fixed human-annotated benchmark of 8,352 pairs, a 15.5M-parameter four-class reasoning-distilled student achieves AUC 0.924 (95% CI [0.918, 0.929]), compared with 0.912 for the four-class label-only student. At Level 2, product-type test-time training (PT-TTT) uses few-shot demonstrations to optimize lightweight category-specific adapters over the frozen student. PT-TTT improves AUC from 0.924 to 0.941 and average precision from 0.920 to 0.940. On a 100K-pair proxy catalog, the distilled student on a single eight-GPU machine is approximately 5,000x faster and 10,000x lower in estimated cost than direct LLM inference.

</details>


### [223] [RegionFed: Federated Learning for Personalized Query Understanding in Heterogeneous Retail Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05403)
*Quoc H. Nguyen,Ali Lafzi,Abhijeet Phatak,Siddharth Pratap Singh,Rohit Upadhyay,Yogananda Domlur Seetharama,Chittaranjan Tripathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RegionFed是一个架构鲁棒的联邦学习框架，完全在梯度层面运作，利用区域梯度与全局梯度的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号来诊断数据异质性、路由个性化策略并自适应控制个性化强度。该方法在T5、RoBERTa和CNN上无需代码修改即可部署，解决了参数级个性化方法在现代Transformer上崩溃的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零售搜索系统服务不同地理区域时面临显著的数据异质性，这同时挑战了隐私保护和模型个性化。标准联邦学习产生牺牲区域性能的全局模型，而现有参数级个性化联邦学习方法由于绑定嵌入和LayerNorm交互，在现代Transformer上灾难性崩溃（T5上准确率低于10%），亟需一种架构鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: RegionFed完全在梯度级别运作，将模型视为可微黑盒。核心创新在于使用区域梯度与全局梯度的ℓ2冲突作为统一信号，该信号同时用于：（1）诊断区域数据异质性；（2）将每个区域路由到最便宜的充分个性化策略；（3）自适应控制个性化强度。由于不依赖参数级操作，可零代码修改部署于任何架构。

Result: 在三个公开数据集（Amazon ESCI、Amazon Reviews、LEAF-FEMNIST）和四种架构（T5-Small、T5-3B、RoBERTa、CNN）上，RegionFed-Meta达到92.27%准确率，与违反隐私的集中式上限（Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04%）几乎持平（差距仅0.23个百分点，在1σ内），同时提供约0.60的差分隐私保证和O(1/√T)收敛速率。在Transformer上相比崩溃的参数级方法获得巨大提升，在CNN上也保持一致改进。

Conclusion: RegionFed证明梯度级的个性化联邦学习能够在不依赖特定架构假设的情况下，有效解决地理数据异质性与隐私保护的矛盾，在多种现代架构上达到接近集中式训练的精度水平，同时维持严格的隐私保证，是一个具有广泛适用性的联邦学习新范式。

Abstract: Retail search systems serve diverse geographic regions with distinct query patterns, vocabularies, and product preferences, creating significant data heterogeneity that challenges both privacy-preserving training and model personalization. Federated learning offers a natural solution for privacy, but standard FL methods produce global models that sacrifice regional performance, while existing personalized FL approaches operate at the parameter level and catastrophically collapse on modern transformers (below 10\% accuracy on T5) due to tied embeddings and LayerNorm interactions. We introduce RegionFed, an \textit{architecture-robust} federated learning framework that sidesteps this failure by operating entirely at the gradient level. RegionFed uses the $\ell_2$ conflict between regional and global gradients as a unified signal that (i) diagnoses heterogeneity, (ii) routes each region to the cheapest sufficient personalization strategy, and (iii) adaptively controls personalization strength. Because it treats models as differentiable black boxes, RegionFed deploys on T5-Small, T5-3B, RoBERTa, and CNN with zero code changes, providing large gains on transformers (where parameter-level methods collapse) and consistent improvements on CNNs. Across three public datasets (Amazon ESCI, Amazon Reviews, LEAF-FEMNIST) and four architectures, RegionFed-Meta achieves 92.27\%, closing the gap to the privacy-violating centralized upper bound (Centralized + Regional Weighting: 92.04\%, $Δ$=0.23pp, within 1$σ$) while providing $(ε{\approx}0.60)$-differential privacy and $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ convergence.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [At Equal Inference Cost, Multi-Agent Structure Does Not Beat a Single Frozen Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04217)
*David Dylan,Aoife Brennan,Cian Murphy,Niamh O'Sullivan,Conor Kelly,Saoirse Walsh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出MA-Evolve方法，在固定语言模型调用总预算下比较多智能体团队与单智能体的演化性能。结果表明，在ALFWorld上，完整团队虽取得最高平均分（0.769 vs 0.754），但与单智能体相比并无统计显著差异（p=0.80），且消耗1.8倍评估调用；价值完全来自执行者，规划者和批评者演化为空提示。在WebShop上演化为空且团队趋势更差。结论：在同等推理成本下，多智能体结构增加成本而无明显收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化方法搜索多智能体LLM管道的角色、拓扑和提示时，往往在相同环境回合数下与单智能体比较，使团队获得额外计算优势。作者希望修正这一不公平比较，在固定语言模型调用次数预算下，检验多智能体团队演化是否仍优于单智能体演化。

Method: 提出MA-Evolve，将Planner-Executor-Critic三人团队表示为三个可演化的角色提示，在共享冻结的7B骨干模型上通过逐角色坐标上升法优化。通过leave-one-in分析探索各角色的实际贡献。

Result: 在ALFWorld，单执行者演化显著优于未演化智能体；完整团队平均分最高（0.769 vs 0.754）但无统计显著差异（p=0.80），消耗1.8倍评估调用。Leave-one-in分析显示价值完全来自执行者，规划者和批评者无实质贡献。即使给团队2-3倍免费计算，也仅能匹敌单智能体。WebShop上团队趋势更差。

Conclusion: 在等量推理成本下，多智能体团队结构相比单智能体仅增加成本而没有明显收益；先前报告的多智能体优势可能主要源于额外的计算预算而非结构本身的价值。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM pipelines, such as Planner-Executor-Critic teams, often report gains over single agents, but these gains usually come with higher inference cost because the team makes multiple model calls per environment step. Existing automated methods search over roles, topologies, and prompts, but typically compare teams against single agents at equal environment rollouts, giving the team extra compute. We instead fix the total number of language-model calls and ask whether evolving a multi-agent team still beats evolving a single agent under the same budget.
  We introduce MA-Evolve, which represents a Planner-Executor-Critic team as three evolvable role prompts and optimizes them by per-role coordinate ascent over a shared frozen 7B backbone. On ALFWorld, evolving a single executor significantly improves over the unevolved agent, while the full team achieves the highest mean but is not statistically better than the single agent: 0.769 versus 0.754, p = 0.80, despite using 1.8 times more evaluation calls. Leave-one-in analysis shows that the realized value comes entirely from the executor; the planner and critic evolve to empty or low-impact prompts and rarely change the executor's action. With 2-3 times free compute, the team only matches the single agent, and on WebShop evolution is null while the team trends worse. Under equal inference cost, multi-agent structure adds cost without clear benefit.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-dimensional Bias in Modeling Multi-dimensional Preferences: Evaluating the Ability of Synthetic Agents to Replace Human Participants in Conjoint Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04243)
*Ho Ting Hung,Nachiket Midha,Victor Y. Wu,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在联合分析实验中（政治学中日益流行的方法）使用LLM生成的合成代理能否复现人类多维偏好模式。通过复制已发表的联合研究，从表征对应性、推断对应性和程序稳定性三个维度比较合成代理与人类数据的结果，发现结果在各维度和研究中表现参差不齐，表明合成参与者的有效性是"声明依赖"和分层的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管利用LLM增强调查实验的鲁棒性或降低数据收集成本引起广泛兴趣，但它们在联合设计（conjoint design）中的有效性仍未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，考察合成代理能否再现联合分析旨在捕捉的多维人类偏好模式。

Method: 复制已发表的联合研究，将合成代理生成的结果与原始人类数据进行比较。评估沿三个维度展开：（1）表征对应性——选择分布的对齐程度；（2）推断对应性——估计值的统计和实质性相似性；（3）程序稳定性。采用综合的复制与比较分析框架。

Result: 结果显示合成代理的表现在这三个维度和所复制的各项研究中并不均匀。通过图表复现或获得较强的符号一致性虽能表明总体输出层面的相似性，但这种程度的一致不足以支持用合成代理替代人类受访者。

Conclusion: 合成参与者的有效性应被视为声明依赖且分层的。在将合成代理视为人类样本的稳健替代品之前，学科整体需要先系统性地绘制这一创新在不同层级上的边界。

Abstract: Despite growing interest in using LLMs to add robustness or reduce data-collection costs in survey experiments, their efficacy in conjoint design---an increasingly popular method in political science---remains underexplored. This paper addresses that gap by investigating whether synthetic agents can reproduce the multi-dimensional human preference patterns that conjoint is designed to capture. It replicates published conjoint studies and compares the results generated by synthetic agents with original human data along three dimensions: representational correspondence, inferential correspondence, and procedural stability. Our analysis evaluates the alignment of choice distributions as well as the statistical and substantive similarity of estimates, and the results are uneven across these dimensions and studies replicated. This implies that the validity of synthetic participants should be considered claim-dependent and hierarchical. Reproducing a figure or obtaining strong sign agreement is evidence of similar aggregate outputs, but not enough to support replacing human respondents. Our results suggest that the discipline as a whole must first map this innovation's boundaries across various levels before considering synthetic agents a robust substitute for human samples.

</details>


### [226] [EVOHARNESSBENCH: Can Your Agents Keep Pace with an Evolving Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04280)
*Zixuan Ke,Vaidehi Patil,Haizhou Shi,Yang Li,Ye Liu,Sarath Shekkizhar,Anurag Koul,Jiayu Wang,Xuan Phi Nguyen,Semih Yavuz,Mohit Bansal,Shafiq Joty*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOHARNESSBENCH基准，用于评估智能体在工具、技能和智能体三种能力轴上的"工具箱演进"（harness evolution）场景。与传统的持续学习基准不同（通常将非平稳性放在任务流中而保持工具箱固定），该基准将非平稳性放在外部提供的工具箱本身。基准包含17个多阶段工具箱流、802个任务、520个工具、42个技能和62个智能体，并评估两种互补设置：部署评估（隔离工具箱扩张时对先前已掌握能力的保留）和自进化适应评估（测试累积经验在新能力引入时是否依然有用）。实验揭示三个持续存在的差距：工具箱扩张本身可导致"工具箱诱发遗忘"；自进化适应的收益在演进阶段、能力轴和环境间不一致；保留与适应可能相互矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM智能体依赖工具箱（工具、可复用技能、专家智能体）来运作，且该工具箱在实际中持续演进。现有智能体的持续学习基准通常将随时间变化的非平稳性放在任务流中而保持工具箱固定，无法捕捉工具箱本身演进带来的挑战。作者希望系统性地研究工具箱演进这一独特挑战——即当外部提供的能力集合不断变化时，智能体能否同时保持先前有效行为并适应新能力。

Method: 作者构建EVOHARNESSBENCH基准：(1) 从基于验证器的基准中确定性构造17个多阶段工具箱流，含802个任务、520个工具、42个技能和62个智能体；(2) 沿工具、技能、智能体三个能力轴施加受控的工具箱演进；(3) 设计两种互补评估设置——部署评估（研究工具箱扩张下对先前已解任务的保留能力）和自进化适应评估（研究累积经验在新能力引入时的可迁移性）。

Result: 实验发现三个持续性差距：(1) 仅工具箱扩张就能降低对先前已解任务的表现，产生"工具箱诱发遗忘"；(2) 自进化适应的增益在工具箱演进阶段、能力轴和不同环境间表现不一致；(3) 保留先前能力并不一定有助于适应新引入能力，反之亦然，表明二者可能相互牵扯。这些结果确立了工具箱演进作为构建能跟随演进工具箱且保持先前有效行为的智能体的独特挑战。

Conclusion: 工具箱演进（harness evolution）应被视为智能体评估与设计中一个独立的、区别于传统任务流非平稳性的重要挑战。当前智能体在保留先前能力与适应新能力之间存在系统性差距且两者可能冲突，需要专门的应对策略。EVOHARNESSBENCH提供了可控、可复现的评测平台来推进这一方向的研究。

Abstract: Modern LLM-based agents operate through a harness of tools, reusable skills, and specialist agents that shapes what they observe and what they can do. In practice, this harness continually evolves as new capabilities are added. We introduce EVOHARNESSBENCH, a benchmark for evaluating agents under controlled harness evolution across three axes (tools, skills, and agents). Unlike existing continual-learning benchmarks for agents, which typically place non-stationarity (i.e., what changes over time) in the task stream while keeping the harness fixed, EVOHARNESSBENCH places non-stationarity in the externally supplied harness itself. It contains 17 multi-stage harness streams constructed deterministically from verifier-based benchmarks, comprising 802 tasks, 520 tools, 42 skills, and 62 agents. We evaluate two complementary settings corresponding to the central challenges of harness evolution: deployment evaluation, which isolates retention of previously accessible competence as the harness expands, and self-evolving adaptation evaluation, which tests whether accumulated experience remains useful as new capabilities are introduced. Our results reveal three persistent gaps. First, harness expansion alone can degrade performance on previously solved tasks, producing harness-induced forgetting. Second, gains from self-evolving adaptation remain inconsistent across stages of harness evolution, capability axes, and environments. Third, retention and adaptation can pull in different directions: preserving earlier competence does not necessarily improve adaptation to newly introduced capabilities, and vice versa. These results establish harness evolution as a distinct challenge for building agents that can keep pace with an evolving harness while preserving previously effective behavior.

</details>


### [227] [Abstraction Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04303)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Abstraction Agent，一种零样本流水线，利用大语言模型从自然语言游戏描述中自动发现连续战略特征、为私有状态打分并聚类形成抽象桶，无需任何游戏特定评估器、训练数据或博弈树遍历。在HUNL转牌局相较期望手牌强度基线降低高达62%的利用性，并在ROVER Trials等游戏上表现优异，且能无缝迁移到多种扑克变体和麻将游戏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息抽象方法依赖领域特定的评估器（如手牌强度计算器或权益估计器），需要专家知识和工程投入，且对大多数研究较少的游戏不可用，严重限制了博弈求解算法在大型不完美信息游戏中的扩展。

Method: 提出四阶段零样本流水线：(1) 基于校准锚点的特征发现；(2) 批处理私有状态打分；(3) 基于相关性的特征选择；(4) k-means聚类。全程由LLM驱动，无需领域评估器、训练数据或博弈树遍历。

Result: 在HUNL转牌局中相对期望手牌强度基线降低高达62%的提升策略可利用性；在ROVER Trials上每个粒度均超越标量排名基线；并以不变提示词成功迁移到四张牌Pot-Limit Omaha、HUNL翻牌前和翻牌以及立直麻将。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了结构化知识抽取的有效性：将LLM参数中蕴含的隐式战略知识转化为显式数值特征，供下游算法计算使用，为缺乏领域工具的博弈提供了通用的抽象构建方案。

Abstract: Information abstraction, which groups strategically similar private states into a tractable number of buckets, is essential for scaling game-solving algorithms to large imperfect-information games. Constructing effective abstractions, however, has traditionally required domain-specific evaluators such as hand-strength calculators or equity estimators, which demand expert knowledge and engineering effort and are unavailable for most less-studied games. We propose the Abstraction Agent, a zero-shot pipeline that uses a large language model (LLM) to discover continuous strategic features from a natural-language game description, score private states on these features, and cluster them into abstraction buckets, without any game-specific evaluator, training data, or game-tree traversal during abstraction construction. The pipeline runs in four phases: feature discovery with calibration anchors, batched private-state scoring, correlation-based feature selection, and $k$-means clustering. The resulting abstractions reduce lifted-strategy exploitability by up to 62% relative to an expected-hand-strength baseline on heads-up no-limit Texas hold'em (HUNL) turn endgames, and beat a scalar rank baseline at every granularity on ROVER Trials, an original game absent from any pretraining corpus. Beyond these quantitative benchmarks, the pipeline transfers with unchanged prompts to four-card Pot-Limit Omaha, HUNL preflop and flop, and Riichi Mahjong, where the discovered features track each game's recognized strategic concepts. This is structured knowledge elicitation: converting implicit strategic knowledge in LLM parameters into explicit numerical features for downstream algorithmic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/lbn187/AbstractionAgent.

</details>


### [228] [Online Change-point Detection for Cooperative Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05298)
*Fatemeh Saberi Khomami,Julita Vassileva*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出PPR（Patterns of Past Rewards），一种轻量级、算法无关的在线变点检测器，用于合作式多智能体强化学习（MARL）系统中检测环境或任务目标的变化。PPR通过平滑智能体的回报流、突出近期变化，并应用统计漂移检测来标记显著偏移。在自定义的Speaker-Listener环境中，实验显示PPR在检测速度与警报稳定性之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合作式MARL系统依赖过去经验学习协调行为，但当环境或任务目标在训练过程中发生变化时，这些经验可能变得不可靠。智能体需要先识别情况已改变，才能决定如何适应，因此研究在线变点检测在MARL中的应用具有必要性。

Method: 提出PPR检测器，核心包括三个步骤：(1)平滑智能体的回报流；(2)突出近期变化；(3)应用统计漂移检测器标记显著偏移。该方法轻量且算法无关，可适配不同MARL算法。

Result: 在基于Multi-Agent Particle Environment的Speaker-Listener环境中，在两种受控非平稳场景下评估，结果显示出检测速度与警报稳定性之间的权衡：平滑回报基线检测较早但产生大量重复警报；直接应用于原始回报的检测器常错过偏移；PPR能在限制冗余检测的同时可靠识别受控偏移。

Conclusion: PPR作为一种轻量级、基于回报的监控工具，能够使合作式MARL系统在训练期间可靠地识别重大变化，为后续的适应策略提供基础支持。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems rely on past experience for learning coordinated behaviour, but this experience may become unreliable if the environment or task objective changes during training. In such cases, agents first need a way to recognize that the situation has changed before deciding how to adapt. This paper studies online change-point detection for cooperative MARL using reward-derived signals. We propose \emph{Patterns of Past Rewards} (PPR), a lightweight algorithm-agnostic detector that smooths agents' return streams, highlights recent changes, and applies a statistical drift detector to flag significant shifts. We evaluate PPR in a custom Speaker-Listener environment based on the Multi-Agent Particle Environment under two controlled non-stationarity scenarios. Our results show a trade-off between detection speed and alarm stability. A smoothed-return baseline detects earlier but produces many repeated alarms. In contrast, applying the detector directly to raw returns often misses the shift. PPR offers a more balanced approach by limiting redundant detections while still identifying the controlled shifts. These findings highlight PPR as a lightweight, reward-based monitoring tool that enables cooperative MARL systems to reliably identify major changes during training.

</details>


### [229] [Trust-Aware Adaptive Disclosure for Inference Privacy Preservation in Multi-Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05340)
*Puspanjali Ghoshal,Tobias J. Oechtering*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体系统中隐私保护的一致性问题，提出了一种基于信任感知的隐私控制框架（Trust-Aware Privacy Control），通过依赖动态信任关系的随机策略控制信息披露，在共识性能与隐私保护之间取得平衡。实验表明该方法能降低对抗性目标推断精度，同时保持有竞争力的共识效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统日益部署于信息关键型系统（如医疗管理、智能电网），其中每个智能体拥有需要向观察者（对手）隐藏的潜在目标。现有的共识机制容易受到目标推断攻击（goal inference attacks），即对手可通过观测通信信息推断智能体的真实目标，因此需要一种能在保证共识性能的同时有效保护智能体隐私的方法。

Method: 提出Trust-Aware Privacy Control框架：1）基于智能体之间动态信任关系（trust relationships）调节消息披露程度；2）采用信任依赖的随机策略（trust-dependent stochastic policy）来控制信息释放；3）在共识性能与隐私保护之间实现可调节的权衡（tradeoff）。

Result: 实验验证表明，与代表性基线方法相比，该方法显著降低了对抗性目标推断（adversarial goal inference）的准确率，同时保持了有竞争力的共识效用（consensus utility），从而证明了信任感知机制在多智能体系统隐私保护中的有效性。

Conclusion: 信任感知的隐私控制机制能够有效保护多智能体系统中的智能体隐私，抵御目标推断攻击，同时在共识性能上不产生显著的牺牲，为信息关键型场景下多智能体系统的安全运行提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Agent based systems are increasingly deployed in information critical systems including healthcare management systems, and smart grids. In this paper, we consider a multi-agent system where each agent has a latent goal that needs to be kept hidden from observing adversaries. More specifically, this paper studies privacy-preserving consensus in networked multi-agent systems under goal inference attacks. We propose a Trust-Aware Privacy Control framework that adapts message disclosure based on the dynamic trust relationships between agents. The proposed method controls information release using a trust-dependent stochastic policy. This enables a tradeoff between consensus performance and privacy preservation. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed method reduces adversarial goal inference accuracy compared to representative baselines, while maintaining competitive consensus utility, thereby highlighting the effectiveness of trust-aware mechanisms in privacy preservation of the agents in multi-agent systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EXAONE Finance是一个专为金融时序预测设计的时序基础模型，采用无注意力架构（因果1D卷积+分组感知池化MLP），通过掩码上下文增强处理金融市场的缺失数据，并在大规模多元金融语料上预训练，在FinVerse基准上达到三个评估维度的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时序基础模型存在三大局限：(1)依赖自注意力机制，计算成本随序列长度和变量数量呈二次方增长；(2)假设输入完全观测，无法处理金融数据中普遍存在的缺失（intermittent observations）；(3)预训练语料未覆盖金融市场的独特动态。这些问题阻碍了现有模型在金融领域的应用，而金融领域恰恰具有长序列、多通道、间歇性观测的特点。

Method: (1)采用无注意力架构，用因果1D卷积替代执行时间维混合，用分组感知池化MLP替代执行变量维混合，两者都是线性时间复杂度的简单算子；(2)引入掩码上下文增强（masked context augmentation），在训练中暴露模型于连续缺失区间，增强对金融数据缺失的鲁棒性；(3)在大规模金融语料上预训练，覆盖股票、外汇、商品、加密资产、固定收益及宏观经济指标等多元资产类别。

Result: 在FinVerse金融预测基准上，EXAONE Finance取得最优性能（state-of-the-art），在三个评估层级（点预测准确率、横截面资产排序、组合盈利能力）均排名第一。

Conclusion: 本文证明专为金融领域设计、采用注意力无关架构并针对缺失数据训练的时序基础模型，能够超越通用时序基础模型在金融预测任务上的表现，验证了领域专用时序基础模型的有效性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [231] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统化叙述性综述梳理了AI招聘从双边检索、行为排名，到神经人岗匹配、LLM组件及工具型招聘智能体的演进历程。基于截至2026年7月和9月的检索与编码协议，组织了40篇代表性和配套工业/法律文献，聚焦三个耦合转变（相似性→互惠适配、模型→复合工作流、离线预测→证据与生产力对齐评估），并提出从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射及未来研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI招聘的自动化对象已从档案对与排名列表扩展到检索证据、比较候选人并支持或执行行动的多阶段工作流，但现有研究与评估框架仍停留在旧范式，缺乏对工作流级证据体系、评估局限及可辩护结论的系统梳理。

Method: 采用有目的的检索与编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，另截至2026年9月2日定向更新），系统化组织40篇代表性文献及配套工业与法律来源，对文档理解、检索、排名、评估、面试、人才寻源及人工交接等环节的证据类型（字段/配对/列表/案例/轨迹/结果层面）进行分类与差距分析。

Result: 识别出三个耦合技术转变；区分了六类证据层面；揭示关键差距：行为标签混淆暴露/偏好/资质、私有与合成数据限制外部效度、最终输出分数掩盖流水线失败、隐私未直接评估且无单一工作联合评估效用/公平/隐私/安全。

Conclusion: 提出从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射，并倡导互惠、证据基础、时间控制、选择性与可审计的系统议程；以是否检索正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议决策并在明确成本与风险约束下改善结果作为进步标准。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [232] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters,一个统一的智能体基准评测基础设施,包含80多个基准适配器、大规模跨模型评测以及精选的Harbor-Index任务集(82个高难度任务,来自29个基准),所有配置通过率均低于30%,最强配置(GPT-5.5与Codex)达到28.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准评测因复杂环境和多样的智能体集成而难以进行,缺乏统一的评测基础设施来进行大规模、可靠的智能体能力评估。

Method: 贡献三部分:(1)开发80多个基准适配器,支持任意智能体的评测,并通过代码审查和一致性实验验证;(2)对8个模型在54个基准上进行大规模评测,每个模型均使用Terminus-2及3个原生框架之一运行;(3)通过难度过滤、AI和人工审计及审计-修复循环,从适配套件中提炼出Harbor-Index(82个任务,覆盖29个基准)。

Result: Harbor-Index保留了大规模评测的挑战性与广度,同时保持可负担的运行成本;没有评测配置超过30%通过率,最强配置(GPT-5.5+Codex)达到28.0%,并发布了适配器、评测结果、深度分析及Harbor-Index开源工件。

Conclusion: Harbor Adapters提供了更可靠、更全面的语言模型智能体评测基础设施,通过开源发布支持社区对智能体能力与失败模式的深入分析。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [233] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用机器学习算法（KNN、RF、SVM）对电力系统突发事件进行安全等级分类（安全、中等、严重三级）。通过Newton-Raphson潮流计算提取数据，以OPI作为安全指标，结合SMOTE（处理类别不平衡）和PCA（降维）四种预处理配置，在IEEE-14和IEEE-30母线上进行N-k（k=1,2,3）情景验证。结果表明RF性能最优（F1达0.97/0.86），PCA对整体性能贡献大于SMOTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统的安全稳定至关重要，尤其是在发生扰动事件时。传统的事故分析（contingency analysis）方法在大规模系统中计算开销大、实时性差。对电力系统突发事件进行有效分类可实现主动决策，避免大规模故障，因此需要开发可扩展、实时、高效的自动化安全评估方法。

Method: 1) 使用Newton-Raphson潮流法从突发事件场景中提取系统数据，采用总体性能指标（OPI）作为安全度量标准。2) 数据预处理采用SMOTE解决类别不平衡问题，PCA进行降维，并构建四种混合预处理配置：归一化、SMOTE均衡、PCA变换、SMOTE+PCA组合。3) 训练KNN、RF和SVM三种分类器，通过N-k情景（k=1,2,3）在IEEE-14和IEEE-30母线系统上生成数据集。4) 以精确率、召回率和F1分数评估性能，重点关注严重事件类别。

Result: RF在IEEE-30和IEEE-14上取得最高F1分数（分别为0.97和0.86）；SVM从PCA中获益显著，提升分类准确率；KNN最适合SMOTE+PCA组合转换。研究表明PCA对整体模型性能的贡献大于SMOTE；SMOTE虽提升召回率但可能引入误报，是准确率上的折中。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为一种可扩展且强大的传统事故分析替代方案，提升电力系统安全性的实时评估能力。研究结果为电力系统安全分类提供了系统的预处理与算法选择指导，强调了不同预处理技术（尤其PCA）与不同分类器的适配性。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [234] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Iris-mini（35B-A3B）和Iris-pro（397B-A17B）两个搜索智能体，介绍了其数据构建、训练流程与SFT-RL交替训练方法，在BrowseComp等基准上取得开源搜索智能体中的最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搜索智能体需要具备多跳推理和检索能力，但高质量训练数据的构建与训练流程缺乏系统性方案；同时推理时的上下文管理对性能影响显著，值得系统研究。

Method: 通过网页超链接结构反向构建多跳问题：从种子页面及出链中提取实体图、将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配、仅保留参考模型闭卷失败但提供证据后可解决的题目。轨迹经轨迹级和回合级过滤后进行SFT，再用RL结合实时搜索优化策略（奖励评判与观察摘要器部署在训练集群内，超长回滚按请求级中断并从已提交前缀恢复）。采用SFT-RL climbing交替两阶段训练，将每轮RL中最难的已解决问题和最高效的回滚输入下一轮监督训练。

Result: 在启用上下文管理时，Iris-mini/Pro在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE上分别达到82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3和88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4，为各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强整体结果。

Conclusion: Iris系列通过系统化的数据构建与SFT-RL交替训练流程，证明了在搜索智能体领域规模化训练的有效性，并计划开源模型权重及完整的数据、训练和评估方案。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [235] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测试时方法来改善大语言模型（LLM）解释的忠实性，特别是针对解释不完整的问题。方法是将解释中未提及的概念从输入中移除后重新查询模型，以消除未被提到的影响因素，同时保留被提及概念的影响。该方法无需修改模型参数，在多个数据集和模型上均优于标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于影响重大的决策，其解释是审计模型行为的重要工具。然而，这些解释可能不忠实——无法反映模型决策背后的真实推理过程。已有训练时方法需要访问模型权重和大量计算资源，而测试时方法主要针对不健全性（引用未影响答案的因素），缺少直接针对不完整性（遗漏影响答案的因素）的方法。

Method: 提出一种测试时方法直接针对解释的不完整性问题：从输入中移除解释中未提及（未归因）的概念，然后用精简后的输入重新查询模型。这样做可以消除未被提及的影响因素，同时保留被提及概念的影响，从而揭示解释中遗漏的隐藏影响因素。

Result: 在两个数据集、多个模型家族和两个独立的忠实性度量指标上，该方法相比标准提示和鼓励忠实性的提示方法，均能改善解释的忠实性。

Conclusion: 该方法无需模型参数修改，与具体模型无关，可在推理时灵活应用，为减少隐藏影响因素、提高LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性提供了有效机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [236] [La Agente Óptima: Towards Agentic Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04564)
*Marcel Müller,Jiaru Bai,Willi Gottstein,Abhijoy Mandal,Mohammad Nazeri,Elia Savino,Yanlin Fang,Sujoy Das,Sergio Pablo García Carrillo,Yeonghun Kang,Juan B. Pérez-Sánchez,Simone Pilon,Martin Fitzner,Timothy Noël,Frank Gu,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了La Agente Óptima，一个基于LLM的智能体框架，用于构建和监督贝叶斯优化活动，将LLM推理与执行分离，使自主实验室（SDLs）的优化活动无需专家干预即可长期运行，并通过多个数字和物理实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主实验室（SDLs）虽能结合自动化实验与自适应决策加速科学发现，但其运行通常依赖人类专家将科学目标转化为可执行的闭环优化活动，并根据数据和条件变化不断调整。这种人工依赖限制了SDL的普及和应用范围。

Method: 提出La Agente Óptima框架，其核心设计是将LLM推理与执行的贝叶斯优化活动相分离：LLM负责初始构建和监督优化活动，而优化循环本身保持持久化状态并由可执行代码重复运行；只有需要解释或修订活动时才将控制权交还给LLM代理，同时保持每个决策的可审计性。

Result: 通过消融研究、五个数字发现任务和两个物理平台进行评估。在闭环接触角优化中，Óptima识别并纠正了运行中的测量失败（接触角从71.4降至67.8度），并正确推断目标不可达、建议更换配方。在为期五天的多目标流动化学活动中，Óptima通过23次实验将产率从30%提升至59%，成本更低且原料消耗大幅减少。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于LLM的智能体能够为领域科学家提供严谨、长期运行的优化活动，无需专家进行专门设置，从而扩大了自主实验室的应用范围。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories (SDLs) combine automated experimentation with adaptive decision-making to accelerate scientific discovery. Their operation nevertheless often depends on human specialists who translate scientific objectives into executable closed-loop campaigns. Specialists adjust them as data and operating conditions change. Here, we present La Agente Óptima, an agentic framework that constructs and supervises Bayesian optimization campaigns across computational and experimental systems while maintaining a persistent optimization state. By separating large language model (LLM) reasoning from executed campaigns, Óptima runs repetitive optimization loops consistently, returns control to the agent only when progress requires interpretation or campaign revision, and keeps every decision auditable. We evaluate Óptima across ablation studies, five digital discovery tasks, and two physical platforms. Throughout, Óptima maintained executable campaigns as both the scientific problem and execution environment evolved. In a closed-loop contact angle optimization campaign, Óptima identified and corrected a mid-run measurement failure, bringing the contact angle from 71.4 to 67.8 degrees, just above the 64-66 degree range. From this result, Óptima correctly inferred that the target was likely unattainable with the available reagents and recommended changing the formulation. In a five-day multi-objective flow-chemistry campaign, Óptima increased the yield from 30% to 59% over 23 experiments. Despite substantial inference costs, it cost less and used substantially less starting material than a human-directed campaign, while selecting a more mass-efficient operating point. These results show that LLM-based agents can make rigorous, long-running optimization campaigns accessible to domain scientists without specialist setup, expanding the scope of SDLs.

</details>


### [237] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了"能力悖论"：提升单个大语言模型的能力可能反而降低系统层面的表现。这是因为共享训练数据和架构使得更强大的LLM行为更加相似，产生无法分散掉的相关性风险。作者通过金融市场智能体模拟验证了这一假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核、招聘等高风险真实系统中，但现有研究多关注个体模型能力提升，缺乏对系统层面结果的分析。作者希望探索个体能力与系统稳定性之间的关系。

Method: 作者构建了一个通用理论框架，展示模型间的相关性如何产生不可分散的风险下限。随后在金融市场中使用具有不同通用能力的LLM交易员进行基于智能体的模拟实验来检验预测。

Result: 研究发现：(1)前沿LLM表现出显著的相关行为，且相关性随能力提升而增加；(2)当共享推理准确时，增加智能体参与度可降低市场风险；(3)当智能体共享错误的错误信息环境时，同样的相关性行为反而成为负债。

Conclusion: 研究识别出"能力悖论"：改进个体模型并不必然带来更好的系统级结果。其他领域是否出现同样动态仍有待实证检验。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [238] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性综述，截至2026年8月31日，系统梳理了大语言模型作为"后果性智能体"的研究证据。作者提出应从"被委派的权威"、"时间持久性"和"环境耦合"三个维度组织证据，并严格区分模型、框架（harness）和环境。结论是行动-接口扩展的证据较为充分，但鲁棒完成任务、恢复、授权和独立验证的证据不足。作者提出"合理化委派"（justified delegation）作为分析与规范启发式框架，并给出相应的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型正在从纯粹的文本生成器转变为能够改变外部状态的智能体，但学术界和产业界常以"走向自主"的单一叙事来描绘这一进展，混淆了模型能力、系统集成、持久性与安全授权等不同概念。作者旨在厘清证据边界，纠正过度宣称，并提供更严谨的分析框架。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，综合一手研究与官方技术规范，按"被委派的权威、时间持久性、环境耦合"三个维度组织证据，并借由"模型-框架-环境"三层分离架构进行分析。在此基础上提出"合理化委派"作为分析与规范启发式：仅在证据支持来源可溯、权限受限、故障检测、安全恢复及人类可控校准的情况下扩大行动范围。

Result: 行动-接口扩展（如工具调用、MCP、Agent2Agent、多智能体组织）的证据较为充分，但鲁棒完成、恢复、授权和独立验证方面证据不足；MCP和Agent2Agent改善互操作性但未建立可信委托；多智能体在增加专业化的同时带来成本和相关失败；持久模拟与世界模型支持训练和规划但本身不证明具有能动性；机器人及自动驾驶实验室展示的是受限可行性而非无人值守的开放世界可靠性。

Conclusion: 作者主张以"合理化委派"替代笼统的"自主性"叙事。据此提出研究议程：耦合模型-框架联合评估、基于能力的权限机制、持久状态管理、跨智能体责任归属，以及分阶段物理验证，以在扩展智能体能力的同时保障安全与可信问责。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [239] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*
