Method: 采用综述研究法，从DLM建模特性（双向性、并行精炼、可控性、质量-延迟弹性）出发，与移动边缘智能体的系统级需求（延迟、内存、能耗、带宽、隐私、可靠性）进行对照分析，覆盖资源高效架构、训练推理加速、压缩、边云部署、通信感知服务、物联网/无线应用及评估等方面。

Result: 识别出DLM在边缘场景中的关键优势（部分精炼、提前退出、约束引导修正可降低响应延迟和通信开销），并指出了当前开放问题：长上下文状态管理、分割推理、可信执行、多模态接地和可复现基准测试等。

Conclusion: DLM的非自回归生成范式为实现灵活的移动边缘智能提供了独特机会，弥合了模型建模特性与系统级约束之间的差距，但仍需解决多个关键技术挑战以实现实际部署。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a non-autoregressive alternative for mobile edge agentic artificial intelligence (AI) by refining tokens through iterative denoising rather than left-to-right decoding. Compared with autoregressive Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), DLMs can update multiple uncertain tokens in parallel and exploit bidirectional context throughout the generation process, enabling more flexible quality-latency trade-offs beyond fixed sequential decoding. These properties are particularly attractive for edge agents, where partial refinement, early exit, and constraint-guided correction can reduce response delay and communication overhead while improving robustness under noisy, incomplete, or dynamic contexts. This survey reviews DLM foundations and analyzes their suitability for edge settings under latency, memory, energy, bandwidth, privacy, and reliability constraints. We cover resource-efficient architectures, training and inference acceleration, compression, edge/cloud deployment, communication-aware serving, Internet of Things (IoT)/wireless applications, and evaluation of DLM-based agents. We further discuss open issues in long-context state management, split inference, trustworthy execution, multimodal grounding, and reproducible benchmarking. The goal is to connect DLM modeling properties, including bidirectionality, parallel refinement, controllability, and quality-latency elasticity, with system-level requirements of future mobile edge intelligence.

</details>


### [273] [DODR: Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04782)
*Weicai Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DODR架构，将自回归大模型的token级概率采样推理重构为潜在空间中的确定性矩阵运算推理图计算。推理状态由语义单元（短语/句子）构成的快照向量表示，每次推理是一步确定性矩阵操作，无token采样。皮尔士三种推理类型（演绎/归纳/溯因）被形式化为三个可训练矩阵算子，并证明了算子集的极小完备性、秩障碍以及推理图的图灵完备性。实验表明演绎损失收敛至1.40e-05，归纳泛化覆盖率达0.9996，溯因解超过随机基线28倍，冻结算子在未见跨域演绎上100%准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型以token级概率采样进行推理，存在三个根本缺陷：错误累积（errors compound across steps）、概率替代必然性（probabilistic sampling cannot guarantee logical necessity）、以及线性链信息瓶颈（linear-chain structure limits information flow）。这促使作者探索一种确定性、非采样、非自回归的推理范式。

Method: DODR架构包含：(1) 语义单元（非token）作为推理原语构建快照向量；(2) 三种皮尔士推理算子：秩亏的演绎算子（信息坍缩）、满秩的归纳算子（信息扩展）、以Moore-Penrose伪逆定义的溯因算子（信息假设）；(3) 证明算子集极小且完备、不存在单一"超算子"同时实现三种类型（秩障碍）、推理图在图灵完备性下通过Banach不动点定理保证收缩回流收敛；(4) 三层持续学习机制及结构性零幻觉保证。

Result: 在503条样本记录（420条去重样本）上的实验结果显示：演绎损失收敛至1.40e-05；归纳达到0.9996的泛化覆盖率并在20/20硬性反例上通过；溯因解超越随机基线28倍，判断准确率分别达72.5%（58/80，95%CI [61.9%,81.1%]）和81.7%（49/60，CI [70.1%,89.4%]）；冻结算子在未见跨域演绎任务上达到100%（60/60）。

Conclusion: DODR提供了一种从概率采样推理向确定性代数推理的范式转换，具备结构性零幻觉保证，暗示复杂逻辑推理可能不应依赖于token级概率生成，而应通过确定性矩阵算子在语义潜在空间中完成。代码和数据已全部开源。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) large language models formulate reasoning as token-level probabilistic sampling, which induces three fundamental defects in complex logical reasoning: error accumulation, probability substituting necessity, and the linear-chain information bottleneck. This paper proposes the Deterministic Operator-Driven Reasoning in Latent Space architecture (DODR), which reconstructs reasoning as reasoning-graph computation in a high-dimensional linear-algebraic space. Reasoning states are represented as snapshot vectors whose primitives are semantic units (phrases or sentences) rather than tokens, and each inference step is a deterministic matrix operation with no token sampling. Peirce's three inference types are formalized as three trainable matrix operators: a rank-deficient deduction operator (information collapse), a full-rank induction operator (information expansion), and an abduction operator defined as the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of deduction (information hypothesizing). We prove that the operator set is minimal and complete given Peirce's trichotomy, that no single "super-operator" can realize all three types (a rank obstruction), and that reasoning graphs are Turing-complete with contractive backflow converging by Banach's fixed-point theorem. Experiments on 503 sample records (420 deduplicated samples) across dedicated and end-to-end settings show: deduction loss converges to 1.40e-05; induction achieves 0.9996 generalization coverage with 20/20 hard vetoes on counterexamples; abduction solutions exceed the random baseline by 28x with judgment accuracies of 72.5% (58/80, Wilson 95% CI [61.9%, 81.1%]) and 81.7% (49/60, CI [70.1%, 89.4%]); frozen operators attain 100% (60/60) on unseen cross-domain deduction. The architecture provides a structural zero-hallucination guarantee and a three-layer continual-learning mechanism. All data and code are released.

</details>


### [274] [ProtLingo: Efficient Protein Language Modeling via Conditional Memory and Expert Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04793)
*Mingrui Li,Sixian Shen,Minzhang Li,Ruiyi Zhang,Kexin Zhang,Jiakai Zhang,Jingyi Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProtLingo是一个高效的蛋白质语言模型框架，通过在预训练的单序列骨干网络上增加条件局部记忆和稀疏专家路由机制，以150M规模的骨干参数在蛋白质适应度预测、FLIP基准和接触预测任务上达到竞争性能，同时在突变效应预测上展现出强大的参数效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质语言模型（PLMs）虽能从无标注序列中建模序列-功能关系，但扩大密集Transformer骨干网络的规模往往带来高昂的计算成本，且并不总能一致性地提升突变敏感的预测性能。因此需要一种更高效的PLM架构。

Method: ProtLingo框架包含两个核心机制：(1) 条件局部记忆模块：将上下文残基表示映射为路由特异的离散编码，将中心化局部窗口组合成语义N-gram地址，检索与重复出现的局部序列上下文相关的可复用残差信号；(2) 稀疏混合专家（MoE）层：将选定的前馈网络块升级为包含共享专家和路由专家的稀疏MoE层，实现残基依赖的计算，同时仅激活部分参数。

Result: 在蛋白质适应度预测、FLIP基准以及监督接触预测实验中，ProtLingo以150M规模骨干网络实现了具有竞争力的性能，在突变效应预测上表现出优异的参数效率，并保持了长距离结构表征能力。

Conclusion: ProtLingo证明了通过条件局部记忆与稀疏专家路由的组合，可以在不依赖大规模密集Transformer的情况下高效地建模蛋白质序列-功能关系，为解决PLM中规模与性能不可兼得的难题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Proteins perform diverse cellular functions, and even single amino-acid substitutions can alter stability, activity, or molecular interactions. Protein language models (PLMs) provide a scalable approach for modeling such sequence--function relationships from unlabeled sequences, but increasing the size of dense Transformer backbones often brings substantial computational cost without consistently improving mutation-sensitive prediction. We introduce ProtLingo, an efficient PLM framework that augments a pretrained single-sequence backbone with conditional local memory and sparse expert routing. ProtLingo maps contextual residue representations into route-specific discrete codes, composes centered local windows into latent $N$-gram addresses, and retrieves reusable residual signals associated with recurring local sequence contexts. In parallel, selected feed-forward blocks are upcycled into sparse Mixture-of-Experts layers with shared and routed experts, enabling residue-dependent computation while activating only a subset of parameters. Experiments on protein fitness prediction, FLIP benchmarks, and supervised contact prediction show that ProtLingo achieves competitive performance with a 150M-scale backbone, including strong parameter efficiency on mutation-effect prediction and preserved long-range structural representations.

</details>


### [275] [Whose record is this? Diagnosing and authorizing record use in personalized multimodal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04801)
*Xinyu Mao,Junsi Li,Chenyang Liu,Haoji Zhang,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了视觉记忆误绑定（VMM）问题——即检索到真实记录却应用到错误的视觉主体上。提出记录授权需满足三个条件：主体存在（P）、记录边有效性（E）和答案支持（S），并构建了3,690例匹配的诊断套件RecordAuth-Diag来评估模型在此问题上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文视觉个性化系统虽然能正确检索记录，但可能将记录错误地应用到了不相关的视觉主体上，导致答案错误甚至存在安全隐患。缺乏对这类"记录-主体错配"问题的系统化定义和诊断工具。

Method: 形式化记录授权为三个条件的合取（P∧E∧S），定义VMM为三者任一违例。通过卡片移除和非ce重新标记来归因失败原因，构建RecordAuth-Diag（3,690例）和基于DAVIS的560例本地化验证集，评估多模型家族，并测试类型化预生成授权与完整的P∧E∧S验证方法。

Result: Gemma-3-4B-IT本地未授权使用率达63.69%，而干净召回仅25.75%。CoViP也仅达26.02%。类型化预生成授权将Qwen卡片暴露率从43.63%降至3.06%，但正向召回从86.26%降至60.90%。完整验证中，类型化授权和top-1相关性的不安全释放分别为6.79%和0.89%，移除的33例不安全案例中27例为支持问题、4例为边缘问题。

Conclusion: 视觉记忆误绑定是现有多模态模型中的普遍问题，支持条件（S）比边缘检查（E）贡献更多的授权决策错误。外观只提供P条件下的E证据，认证主体令牌可补充缺失的主体存在证明。研究声明限于评估的协议范围，不涉及自然流行率、同意或视觉身份问题。

Abstract: Contextualized visual personalization can retrieve a true record yet apply it to the wrong visual subject. We formalize when a record may condition an answer as \emph{record authorization}: subject presence ($P$), record-edge validity ($E$), and answer support ($S$) must all hold. We call violations visual memory misbinding (VMM). We construct RecordAuth-Diag, a 3,690-case matched diagnostic suite that changes one image--record edge while holding the query, question, record text, and image multiset fixed. Card removal and nonce relabeling attribute these failures to supplied records. Raw-bank failures span Qwen-, Phi-, and Gemma-family interfaces: Gemma-3-4B-IT reaches 63.69\% local unauthorized use at 25.75\% clean recall. CoViP remains at 26.02\%, versus 22.49\% for its Qwen backbone at similar clean recall. Typed pre-generation authorization reduces Qwen card exposure on RecordAuth-Diag from 43.63\% to 3.06\%, while positive recall changes from 86.26\% to 60.90\%. Full $P\wedge E\wedge S$ validation uses 560 localized DAVIS cases: top-1 relevance and typed authorization have comparable release (28.93\% and 28.39\%) but 6.79\% and 0.89\% unsafe release, respectively. Of the 33 additional unsafe cases removed, 27 are support, 4 edge, 2 clean, and 0 boundary cases. Thus the observed increment is an $E\wedge S$ decision dominated by support, not an edge check alone. Appearance supplies $E$ evidence only conditional on $P$; authenticated subject tokens instantiate the missing presence witness as a sufficiency control. The claims concern the evaluated contracts, not natural prevalence, consent, or visual identity

</details>


### [276] [Hierarchical Possession-Aware Graph Pointer Network for Pass Receiver Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04803)
*Jingyi Wang,Da Li,Kaixin Wang,Zhangqin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对足球传球接收者预测任务，提出了层级持球感知图指针网络（HPGPN），在广播级冻结帧观测（仅部分可见球员、无稳定身份）下，通过图建模球员交互、结合固定事件上下文与动态持球历史，实现变长可见队友候选的接收者预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传球接收者预测是足球分析的基础任务，但现有方法难以应对事件中心冻结帧观测的广播级设置——该设置仅提供部分且可变的球员可见性，缺乏完整轨迹和稳定球员身份。模型必须在部分观测下对匿名可见候选、对手压力及近期上下文进行推理，这给预测带来了显著挑战。

Method: HPGPN将传球接收者选择建模为对可见队友的变长候选预测问题。方法包括：(1) 用图表示当前传球情境，建模球员间的交互；(2) 融入固定事件上下文；(3) 利用动态持球历史刻画进攻序列的演变过程；(4) 通过层级方式整合空间、上下文与历史证据来精化候选表示；(5) 采用glimpse指针头对接收者候选进行打分。

Result: 在公开足球事件和冻结帧数据上的实验表明，HPGPN提升了传球接收者预测性能。消融研究验证了图交互建模、固定事件上下文以及双分支动态持球历史建模各自的有效性。

Conclusion: HPGPN通过层级建模空间、上下文与历史证据，有效解决了广播级部分观测下的传球接收者预测问题，为该任务提供了新的方法框架。

Abstract: Pass receiver selection is a fundamental task in football analytics, aiming to predict the intended receiver under a given game state. This task is challenging with event-centered freeze-frame observations, a broadcast-like setting that provides only partial and variable player visibility without complete trajectories or stable player identities. The model must therefore reason over anonymous visible candidates, opponent pressure, and recent context under partial observation. To address this setting, we propose a Hierarchical Possession-aware Graph Pointer Network (HPGPN), which formulates pass receiver selection as variable-size candidate prediction over visible teammates. HPGPN jointly models current player interactions, local event context, and possession-level temporal dynamics. It represents the current pass situation with a graph, incorporates fixed event context, and uses dynamic possession history to capture how the attacking sequence evolves. Candidate representations are refined hierarchically by integrating spatial, contextual, and historical evidence, and a glimpse pointer head scores the receiver candidates. Experiments on public football event and freeze-frame data show that HPGPN improves pass receiver selection performance. Ablation studies demonstrate the effectiveness of graph-based interaction modeling, fixed event context, and dual-branch dynamic possession-history modeling.

</details>


### [277] [MedFlow: Class-Aware Multi-Scale Generation for Medical Time-Series Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04804)
*Yanhao Huang,Shibo Feng,Wanjin Feng,Peilin Zhao,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedFlow是一个类感知的多尺度流匹配框架，用于合成医学时间序列数据。它通过向量量化的多尺度分词器捕捉不同时间分辨率的临床模式，并引入Token边际引导机制来增强少数类特征，在四个公开数据集上优于现有扩散基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学时间序列合成方法主要关注匹配整体分布和时间动态，但在不平衡的医学数据上无法保证下游任务的实用性。临床信息模式常出现在异质的时间尺度上，而罕见的少数类特征容易被主导群体模式掩盖。

Method: MedFlow包含两个关键组件：1) 向量量化的多尺度分词器，以互补的时间分辨率表示医学序列，捕捉粗粒度临床趋势和细粒度动态；2) Token边际引导机制，将类条件token统计直接融入流匹配过程，引导生成朝向类别特定区域，增强少数类模式同时保留全局和尾部分布。

Result: 在电子健康记录、EEG和ECG信号的四个公开数据集上，MedFlow在下游预测任务中一致优于最新的基于扩散的基线方法。平均AUPRC提升5.8%，Context-FID降低88.6%，采样吞吐量提高3.8倍。

Conclusion: MedFlow通过类感知的多尺度生成策略有效解决了医学时间序列合成中少数类模式被掩盖的问题，在数据质量、下游任务性能和采样效率方面均实现了显著提升，为不平衡医学数据合成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Synthetic medical time-series generation can alleviate data scarcity and support the development of reliable clinical prediction models. However, existing methods mainly focus on matching the overall distribution and temporal dynamics of real data, which does not necessarily ensure strong downstream utility on imbalanced medical datasets. Clinically informative patterns often occur at heterogeneous temporal scales, while rare minority-class characteristics can be obscured by dominant population patterns. To address these challenges, we propose MedFlow, a class-aware multi-scale flow matching framework for medical time-series synthesis. MedFlow employs a vector-quantized multi-scale tokenizer to represent medical sequences at complementary temporal resolutions, capturing both coarse clinical trends and fine-grained dynamics. We further introduce Token Marginal Guidance, which incorporates class-conditional token statistics directly into the flow matching process to steer generation toward class-specific regions of the learned tokens. This mechanism strengthens minority-class patterns, while preserving the global and tail distributions of real data. Experiments on four public datasets covering electronic health records, EEG, and ECG signals demonstrate that MedFlow consistently outperforms recent state-of-the-art diffusion-based baselines across downstream prediction tasks. On average, it improves AUPRC by 5.8%, reduces Context-FID by 88.6%, and achieves 3.8$\times$ higher sampling throughput.

</details>


### [278] [When Financial Fine-tuning Fails: A Three-Level Detectability Analysis of Numerical Hallucination in Domain-Adapted Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04806)
*Xiaodong Li,Peiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对金融大语言模型在摘要生成中的数值幻觉进行了受控研究，发现领域微调显著降低数值约束能力（幻觉率从5.4%升至98%），且数值推理能力的增强反而加剧幻觉。核心机制是模板注入——模型记忆并插入规范数值而忽略输入内容。研究建议评估协议应覆盖所有可检测性级别，并采用接地感知生成或弃权机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融大语言模型在报告和披露摘要中广泛部署，数值幻觉带来重大实际风险。以往研究通常将幻觉归因于数值推理能力不足，但这一假设从未在受控微调条件下被系统性验证。作者旨在通过严谨的实验设计检验这一常见假设是否成立。

Method: 采用低成本受控研究设计，构建三种模型变体：基础指令微调模型(Base)、领域语言适配模型(FT-A)、以及数值增强领域模型(FT-A+B+C)。引入三级可检测性分类法：显性幻觉(货币计价的捏造)、隐性显式幻觉(专业惯例数字)、隐性隐式幻觉(无依据的定量声明)，对金融摘要任务进行系统评估。

Result: 基础模型保持接近零的幻觉率(5.4%)，而FT-A显性幻觉率达82.5%，FT-A+B+C达到98%。数值监督不但没有缓解幻觉，反而在所有级别上放大了幻觉。识别出模板注入（插入记忆的规范值而不考虑输入内容）是微调模型的主要幻觉机制。

Conclusion: 金融摘要中的数值幻觉由领域适配导致的数值约束能力退化驱动，而非数值推理能力不足所致。建议评估协议应覆盖所有可检测性级别的幻觉，部署实践应包含接地感知生成或弃权的明确机制。

Abstract: Financial large language models are increasingly deployed for summarization of reports and disclosures, where numerical hallucination poses significant practical risks. While prior work often attributes such hallucination to insufficient numerical reasoning, this assumption has not been systematically tested under controlled fine-tuning settings. In this paper, we conduct a cost-effective, controlled study of numerical hallucination in financial summarization across three model variants: a base instruction-tuned model, a domain language-adapted model (FT-A), and a numeracy-enhanced domain model (FT-A+B+C). We introduce a three-level detectability taxonomy distinguishing between overt hallucination (currency-denominated fabrication), covert-explicit hallucination (professional-convention numbers), and covert-implicit hallucination (ungrounded quantitative claims). Our results reveal that domain fine-tuning substantially degrades numerical restraint at all detectability levels. While the Base model maintains near-zero hallucination rates (5.4\%), FT-A exhibits 82.5\% overt hallucination and FT-A+B+C reaches 98\%. Contrary to intuition, numeracy supervision amplifies rather than mitigates hallucination across all levels. We identify template injection---the insertion of memorized canonical values regardless of input content---as a primary hallucination mechanism in fine-tuned models. These findings demonstrate that numerical hallucination in financial summarization is driven by the degradation of numerical restraint through domain adaptation, not by insufficient numerical reasoning. We recommend that evaluation protocols assess hallucination across all detectability levels and that deployment practices include explicit mechanisms for grounding-aware generation or abstention.

</details>


### [279] [CPR-IE:A Compression-Prediction-Resource Intelligence Efficiency Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04809)
*Xiantao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出压缩-预测-资源智能效率（CPR-IE）框架，用于在部署约束下按表征经济性、预测质量和资源负担对智能系统进行协议相对排序。通过比例增量组合与上下文无关比率响应推导出对数累积负担和幂律响应，并证明了多项理论性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖预测准确度来比较受部署约束的智能系统是不够的。需要一种更全面的协议相对排序机制，同时考虑表征经济性、预测质量和资源负担三个维度。

Method: 将问题分解为两个子问题：原始资源消耗如何被表示，以及由此产生的属性如何聚合。比例增量组合唯一产生对数累积负担，上下文无关比率响应产生幂律响应，并结合参考归一化得到I(C,P,T)表示。进一步通过translog父模型使交互限制显式化。

Result: 证明了帕累托一致性、单位不变性、边界行为、贸易恒等式、排序稳定性区域和跨任务聚合等性质；建立了基数和序数识别、亚高斯有限样本排序保证、指数不确定性下的稳健选择和确定性遗憾界。

Conclusion: CPR-IE是一种构建的效率表示，而非普适定律或智能本身的定义。MDL、算法复杂度、适当评分规则、变分推断和Landauer原理可用于激励测量选择，但并不蕴含该公式。

Abstract: Comparing intelligent systems under deployment constraints requires more than predictiveaccuracy.This paper develops Compression-Prediction-Resource Intelligence Efficiency (CPR-IE) as a protocol-relative ordering by representational economy, predictive quality, and resourceburden. The analysis separates two questions-how raw resource consumption is represented, andhow the resulting attributes are aggregated. Proportional-increment composition uniquely yieldslogarithmic cumulative burden, and context-independent ratio response yields power responsesto compression, prediction, and burden; with reference normalization the representation is I(C,P,T).We prove Pareto consistency, unit invariance, boundary behavior, trade-off identities, ranking-stability regions, and cross-task aggregation. A translog parent model makes interaction restrictions explicit, and further results establish cardinal and ordinal identification, sub-Gaussianfinite-sample ranking guarantees, robust selection under exponent uncertainty, and deterministicregret bounds. Minimum description length, algorithmic complexity, proper scoring rules, varia-tional inference, and Landauer's principle motivate measurement choices but do not entail theformula. CPR-IE is a constructed efficiency representation, not a universal law or a definition ofintelligence itself.

</details>


### [280] [Long Horizon Transformer Quantile Fault Prediction for Multi Site Industrial Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04840)
*David J Poland,Daniele Ravi,Na Helian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TQRNN30d框架，结合双阶段分位数回归神经网络特征提取器与多流时间融合分类器，用于30天长期视角的预测性维护。在9个制造工厂72台机器的机级分离测试中，30天窗口取得79.97% F1与0.820 ROC-AUC，在7/14/30天固定阈值比较中全面领先18个基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程预测性维护需要区分缓慢演变的退化与正常工况变化，时间窗口以天而非小时计。传统模型在如此长的规划窗口上难以有效区分缓慢退化信号与正常工况波动，作者希望探索显式条件分位数表示能否为这一长期问题提供更具信息量的分类器接口。

Method: TQRNN30d由两部分组成：(1) 双阶段分位数回归神经网络(QRNN)特征提取器，将81通道每小时的机器行为映射为324维分位数状态表示；(2) 多流时间融合分类器，融合分位数状态、动态协变量、通道级静态元数据及168小时潜在历史流，采用门控残差处理、因果循环编码和元数据条件跨模态注意力。另设计有界不稳定性感知信号，基于单步预测误差发散提供最长时程的辅助记忆调制。

Result: 在43/14/15机级分离训练/验证/测试划分下（72台机器、9个工厂），30天窗口达到79.97% F1、80.18%召回率、81.82%精确率、82.39%准确率和0.820 ROC-AUC。在7/14/30天固定阈值比较中全面领先18个基线，其中14天F1优势最大。

Conclusion: 结果表明在该同质九工厂机群内，模型在留出机器上表现良好，支持held-out-machine性能。但研究未证明对未见站点、跨设备类型或跨行业的泛化能力，泛化边界需后续研究验证。

Abstract: Long-horizon predictive maintenance requires models to distinguish slowly evolving degradation from normal operating-regime variation over planning windows measured in days rather than hours. This paper evaluates whether an explicit conditional-quantile representation provides an informative classifier interface for this problem. The proposed TQRNN30d framework combines a dual-stage quantile regression neural network (QRNN) feature extractor with a multi-stream temporal fusion classifier. Each hourly word of 81-channel machine behaviour is mapped to a 324-dimensional quantile-state representation, and 720 ordered hourly words form the 30-day document supplied to the long-horizon model. The classifier fuses quantile states with dynamic covariates, channel-level static metadata, and a 168-hour latent-history stream using gated residual processing, causal recurrent encoding, and metadata-conditioned cross-modal attention. A bounded instability-aware signal derived from sustained one-word-ahead prediction-error divergence provides auxiliary memory modulation at the longest horizon. Evaluation uses a machine-disjoint 43/14/15 train/validation/test allocation across 72 machines in nine manufacturing facilities. At 30 days, TQRNN30d achieves 79.97% F1, 80.18% recall, 81.82% precision, 82.39% accuracy, and 0.820 ROC-AUC. It leads all 18 evaluated baselines at the 7-, 14-, and 30-day fixed-threshold comparisons, with the largest F1 advantage at 14 days. The results support held-out-machine performance within the observed homogeneous nine-facility fleet, but do not establish unseen-site, cross-equipment, or cross-sector generalisation.

</details>


### [281] [ElderBench: Benchmarking Autonomous Mobile Agents for Older Adults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04850)
*Weide Zhan,Qumu Shaqu,Yuanqing Liu,Peng Zhang,Jiahao Liu,Kam Him Lam,Ning Gu,Zhan Hu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ElderBench，这是首个面向老年人在真实场景中自然表达的移动GUI智能体基准测试，包含从20个应用中249个任务，揭示老年用户语言模式（间接表达、指代模糊、请求不明确）导致主流GUI智能体性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI基准主要依赖明确、目标导向的指令，未能捕捉老年人自然出现的语言模式（如间接言语、指代歧义、请求不明确），导致基准指令与真实老年用户交互之间存在不匹配，阻碍了智能体的可靠部署。

Method: 从20个应用中收集249个由老年人自然引发的智能手机任务构建ElderBench；从句法、语义和语用三个视角刻画老年指令与现有GUI基准指令的语言差异；在在线和离线设置下评估主流GUI智能体和视觉语言模型；通过受控指令标准化、失败分析和细粒度语言特征分析，识别导致智能体失败的老年特定语言模式。

Result: 主流GUI智能体和VLM在处理老年导向指令时表现出显著的性能下降；通过分析识别出老年特定语言模式如何导致智能体失败。

Conclusion: 研究提供了面向更具适应性、可解释性和年龄包容性的GUI智能体的可操作设计见解，以更好地服务老年用户。

Abstract: While autonomous mobile agents hold great potential for assisting older adults with smartphone usage, existing GUI benchmarks mainly rely on explicit, goal-oriented instructions and rarely capture the naturally occurring language patterns of older users, such as indirect speech, referential ambiguity, and under-specified requests. This mismatch between benchmark instructions and real-world elderly interactions may hinder reliable agent deployment. To address this gap, we present ElderBench, the first benchmark for evaluating mobile GUI agents in authentic elderly-oriented scenarios. ElderBench is constructed from 249 naturally elicited smartphone tasks collected from older adults across 20 applications. We first characterize the linguistic divergence between elderly instructions and existing GUI benchmark instructions from syntactic, semantic, and pragmatic perspectives. We then evaluate mainstream GUI agents and Vision-Language Models under both online and offline settings, revealing substantial performance degradation when handling elderly-oriented instructions. Through controlled instruction normalization, failure analysis, and fine-grained linguistic feature analysis, we further identify how elderly-specific language patterns contribute to agent failures. Our findings provide actionable design insights toward more adaptive, interpretable, and age-inclusive GUI agents for older adults.

</details>


### [282] [MM-IFEval-Pro: A Multilingual and Attack-Resistant Benchmark for Instruction-Following in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04859)
*Changming Xiao,Zhenliang Ni,Jinhui He,Han Shu,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MM-IFEval-Pro，一个覆盖中英双语及对抗性指令劫持场景的多模态指令跟随基准，包含4大类任务24个子类别和8类指令52个子类别，每个样本平均3.0个约束；同时构建含中文和对抗指令的强化学习训练集，显著提升模型在该基准上的表现，并有效迁移至其他主流多模态基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态指令跟随基准存在语言覆盖有限和对抗性安全场景不足的问题，难以评估真实世界中多语言和安全敏感场景下的模型表现。

Method: 构建MM-IFEval-Pro基准，涵盖中英文任务与多种指令劫持案例，包含4大任务类和24子类，8个指令类和52个子类；并构建富含中文和对抗指令的强化学习训练集以提升模型性能。

Result: 该训练集显著提升了模型在MM-IFEval-Pro上的性能，并有效迁移至其他主流多模态基准，展现了强大的跨任务和跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: MM-IFEval-Pro弥补了现有基准在语言多样性和对抗安全性方面的不足，展现了强化学习训练在多语言、多模态指令跟随中的有效性与泛化性。

Abstract: As vision-language models (VLMs) rapidly advance in image understanding, cross-modal reasoning, and complex instruction execution, instruction-following capability has become a key indicator of their reliability and practicality. However, existing multimodal instruction-following benchmarks still suffer from limited language coverage and insufficient adversarial safety scenarios, making them inadequate for evaluating real-world multilingual and safety-sensitive settings. To address these gaps, we present MM-IFEval-Pro, a multimodal instruction-following benchmark covering Chinese and English tasks as well as diverse instruction hijacking cases. MM-IFEval-Pro includes 4 major task categories and 24 subcategories and 8 instruction categories with 52 subcategories, with each sample containing an average of 3.0 constraints to realistically simulate complex instruction scenarios. We further construct a reinforcement-learning training set enriched with Chinese and adversarial instructions, which significantly improves model performance on MM-IFEval-Pro and transfers effectively to other mainstream multimodal benchmarks, demonstrating strong cross-task and cross-language generalization.

</details>


### [283] [MZ-Rain: Moisture-Budget-Guided Zero-Inflated Model for Station-Level Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04864)
*Yifang Zhang,Shengwu Xiong,Henan Wang,Wenjie Yin,Yuqiang Zhang,Chen Zhou,Hua Chen,Qile Zhao,Pengfei Duan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MZ-Rain，一种基于水分收支方程引导的零膨胀sLSTM框架，用于站点级降水临近预报。通过将降水形成过程分解为水分储存、水分输送、地表蒸发和降水持续性等特定路径，并采用自适应Tweedie建模策略处理降水零膨胀问题，在多个地理和气候区域的实验中优于强基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 站点级降水临近预报对农业、水资源管理和灾害预防至关重要，但传统时间序列建模面临两大挑战：(1)缺乏物理引导建模，将气象变量视为同质集而忽略其在降水形成中的不同作用，导致预测偏离实际物理过程；(2)严重的零膨胀问题，干燥时段主导数据集，掩盖了有意义的降水模式并使建模复杂化。

Method: MZ-Rain框架受水分收支方程引导，将降水形成过程分解为对应水分储存、水分输送、地表蒸发和降水持续性的过程特定路径，并通过专门的sLSTM分支捕捉其时间演化。为处理降水的零膨胀特性，引入自适应Tweedie建模策略，自适应调节降水均值，同时将降水发生作为辅助任务联合学习，以更好平衡干湿判别与定量降水估计。

Result: 在多样化的地理和气候区域进行的广泛实验中，MZ-Rain在多个评估指标（包括CSI、FAR、MSE和MAE）上持续优于强基线方法。特别地，该模型在预报强降水事件方面展现出卓越能力，并受益于基于物理的过程建模。

Conclusion: MZ-Rain通过物理引导的过程分解和零膨胀感知的统计建模，有效提升了站点级降水临近预报的准确性，尤其在强降水事件预测上表现突出，验证了物理引导建模方法在降水预报中的价值。

Abstract: Accurate station-level precipitation nowcasting is critical for agriculture, water resource management, and disaster prevention, which typically is formulated as a time series forecasting problem. However, conventional time-series modeling techniques face two major challenges in addressing station-level precipitation nowcasting: (1) Lack of Physics-Guided Modeling}, where meteorological variables are treated as a homogeneous set without accounting for their distinct roles in precipitation formation, leads to predictions that deviate from the physical processes governing precipitation. (2) Severe zero inflation in precipitation, where dry intervals dominate the dataset, obscuring meaningful precipitation patterns and complicating the predictive modeling. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{MZ-Rain}, a moisture-budget-guided zero-inflated sLSTM framework for station-level precipitation nowcasting. Guided by the moisture budget equation, MZ-Rain decomposes the precipitation formation process into process-specific pathways corresponding to moisture storage, moisture transport, surface evaporation, and precipitation persistence, and captures their temporal evolution through dedicated sLSTM branches. To account for the zero-inflated nature of precipitation, MZ-Rain introduces an adaptive Tweedie modeling strategy that adaptively modulates the rainfall mean while jointly learning precipitation occurrence as an auxiliary task, enabling the model to better balance dry-wet discrimination and quantitative precipitation estimation. Extensive experiments across diverse geographical and climatic regimes demonstrate that MZ-Rain consistently outperforms strong baselines on multiple evaluation metrics, including CSI, FAR, MSE, and MAE. In particular, the model exhibits superior skill in forecasting heavy precipitation events, while benefiting from physically grounded process modeling.

</details>


### [284] [CoSkill: Joint Reinforcement Learning of Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents for Hierarchical Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04865)
*Jinyuan Feng,Dongmin Li,Yiqun Chen,Yang Gao,Xing Chen,Huimu Wang,Zhiqiang Pu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSkill，一个统一的多智能体强化学习框架，将静态的元技能工作流重塑为可学习的元技能智能体，并与推理智能体在分层技能库上联合训练，实现技能与推理的端到端协同适应，在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别达到98.4%和90.6%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库范式的结构性缺陷：要么将技能进化与策略优化解耦，要么将元技能实例化为固定工作流。二者都将技能视为被动管理的对象，限制了技能的灵活进化及其与推理智能体的协同适应。

Method: CoSkill是一个统一的多智能体RL框架，将静态元技能工作流重构为可学习的元技能智能体，并与推理智能体在分层技能库上联合训练。推理智能体与元技能智能体共享单一骨干网络、构成合作团队，推理智能体基于检索到的任务技能及从其子集选出的步骤技能来条件化动作，同时任务表现引导元技能智能体细化步骤技能。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，CoSkill大幅超越先前的技能基与RL基线，成功率分别达到98.4%和90.6%，相比基线分别提升+3.5和+6.2个百分点；同时实现优越的早期样本效率、渐近性能和墙钟效率。

Conclusion: 通过将技能视为与推理智能体共同进化的学习主体而非被动管理对象，CoSkill验证了技能与推理端到端协同适应的有效性，为大规模语言模型智能体的技能重用与演化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Skill libraries improve the sample efficiency of agentic reinforcement learning (RL) by enabling large language model (LLM) agents to reuse procedural knowledge. Yet existing paradigms exhibit structural shortcomings: they either decouple skill evolution from policy optimization or instantiate meta-skills as fixed workflows. Both treat skills as passive objects to be managed, limiting the flexible evolution of skills and their co-adaptation with the reasoning agent. To address the limitations, we propose CoSkill, a unified multi-agent RL framework that recasts the static meta-skill workflow as a learnable Meta-Skill Agent and jointly trains it with a Reasoning Agent over a hierarchical skill library. By modeling the Reasoning and Meta-Skill Agents as a cooperative team sharing a single backbone, CoSkill enables end-to-end co-adaptation: the Reasoning Agent conditions its actions on a retrieved task skill and step skills selected from its child set, while its task performance guides the Meta-Skill Agent in refining those step skills. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop show that CoSkill substantially outperforms prior skill-based and RL baselines, achieving success rates of 98.4% and 90.6%, respectively (+3.5 and +6.2 pp). As shown in Figure 1, CoSkill achieves superior early-stage sample efficiency, asymptotic performance, and wall-clock efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/jinyuan-cookie/CoSkill.

</details>


### [285] [LLM-Assisted Behavioural and Scenario Augmentation for Agent-Based Energy Adoption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04866)
*Iias Faiud,Hossein Khaleghy,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合框架，将大语言模型（LLM）辅助的规格设计整合到爱尔兰奶牛场太阳能光伏（PV）采纳的校准智能体模型（ABM）中，通过有界行为量表和结构化场景规格来增强能源政策模拟分析，既保留了原有技术经济采纳机制，又增加了可解释的行为调控和情景不确定性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能源政策模拟中，直接使用LLM推理替换采纳模型会引发可解释性、可重复性和行为有效性方面的担忧。需要在保留传统ABM可解释性的同时，引入LLM来支持结构化行为假设和探索性技术经济情景分析。

Method: 提出混合框架，整合有界行为量表（保守、平衡、乐观三种可解释类别）和结构化情景规格（固定且经过规则验证）到校准的ABM中。保留原始技术经济采纳机制，通过有界行为调制和场景驱动的蒙特卡洛不确定性分析进行增强。

Result: 实验覆盖多种政策设置、蒙特卡洛世界和随机种子，结果表明采纳结果在行为体制间保持有界且单调，行为采纳相对逻辑回归情况最多增加约13%，且未产生不稳定或不现实的饱和动态。

Conclusion: LLM辅助规格可以以受控、可重复且与政策相关的方式整合到校准的能源ABM中，为模拟能源政策分析提供新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) create opportunities to enrich simulation-based energy policy analysis, particularly by supporting structured behavioural assumptions and exploratory techno-economic scenarios. However, directly replacing adoption models with LLM reasoning raises concerns regarding interpretability, reproducibility, and behavioural validity. This paper proposes a hybrid framework for LLM-assisted specification design, integrating bounded behavioural rubrics and structured scenario specifications into a calibrated agent-based model (ABM) of solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption by Irish dairy farms. The proposed approach preserves the original techno-economic adoption mechanism while augmenting it with bounded behavioural modulation and scenario-driven uncertainty analysis. Behavioural effects are represented through interpretable conservative, balanced, and optimistic rubrics, while future policy and market conditions are explored through fixed, rule-validated scenario specifications. Experimental results across multiple policy settings, Monte Carlo worlds, and random seeds demonstrate stable and economically plausible behaviour, with adoption outcomes remaining bounded and monotonic across behavioural regimes. The framework achieves up to approximately 13% behavioural adoption increase relative to the corresponding logistic case without producing unstable or unrealistic saturation dynamics. The results demonstrate that LLM-assisted specifications can be integrated into calibrated energy ABMs in a controlled, reproducible, and policy-relevant manner.

</details>


### [286] [From Interaction Traces to Persistent Skills: Online Evolution for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04869)
*Longtao Hu,Xiao Liang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个在线技能演化框架(Skill-Evo4GUI)，将交互轨迹和评估反馈转化为持久化、可版本化的可复用程序库，使计算机使用智能体在推理时无需改变模型参数即可持续积累与复用程序性知识。在四个OSWorld应用域中，经过五轮空库预热后，完整演化库系统相比无技能空库对照组平均评估分数高出5.7~18.6个百分点，表明演化技能库可作为可审计、可共享的程序性记忆提升固定计算栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体在图形界面中执行任务时，其交互经验通常是瞬时的——单次rollout获得的程序性知识不会被系统性地保留、精炼并复用于后续任务。现有技能库虽然能提供外部程序性知识，但其相对于无技能智能体的增量价值以及重复交互下的纵向动态特性尚未被充分刻画。

Method: 提出一种在线技能演化框架：1) 将交互轨迹和评估者反馈转换为持久的、带版本的可复用程序库；2) 每次迭代在冻结的库快照上执行，基于证据的技能更新在后续迭代中可用，且不修改模型参数；3) 在四个OSWorld应用域中，将完整的演化库系统与配置匹配的空库对照组进行比较，固定相同的动作生成与GUI grounding栈、任务集和迭代轮次。

Result: 经过五轮空库预热后，完整系统在全部四个域的后预热平均评估分数均更高，平均差异为5.7至18.6个百分点，且存在域相关的时序稳定性。在GIMP域中，溯源分析揭示了跨源任务的检索和修订动荡：反复接受的编辑未能恢复其源任务。

Conclusion: 演化技能库可作为可审计、共享的程序性记忆来改进固定计算机使用栈，但其收益是有条件的，且反复修订并不能保证源任务的恢复。代码已开源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can execute increasingly complex tasks in graphical interfaces, but their interaction experience is typically transient: procedural knowledge acquired from one rollout is not systematically retained, refined, and reused in later tasks. Existing skill libraries provide external procedural knowledge, yet their incremental value over the same agent operating without skills, as well as their longitudinal dynamics under repeated interaction, remain insufficiently characterized. We present an online skill-evolution framework that converts interaction trajectories and evaluator feedback into a persistent, versioned library of reusable procedures. Each iteration executes against a frozen library snapshot, and evidence-guided skill updates become available in subsequent iterations without changing model parameters. We compare the full evolving-library system with a configuration-matched empty-library control across four OSWorld application domains under the same fixed action-generation and GUI-grounding stack, task sets, and iteration horizons. Following a five-iteration empty-library warm-up, Full attains a higher post-warm-up mean evaluator score in all four observed domain runs, with mean differences ranging from 5.7 to 18.6 percentage points and domain-dependent temporal stability. In GIMP, provenance-aware analysis reveals retrieval across task-of-origin boundaries and revision churn, where repeated accepted edits fail to recover the originating task. These findings characterize evolving skill libraries as auditable, shared procedural memory that can improve a fixed computer-use stack, while showing that their benefits are conditional and repeated revision does not guarantee recovery. Code is released at https://github.com/LongtaoHu/Skill-Evo4GUI.

</details>


### [287] [CHAMP: Cross-domain Hybrid Architecture for Matchmaking and Prediction in Online Multi-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04870)
*Kai Wang,Ge Fan,Chaoyun Zhang,Yuyang Jiang,Yuze Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAMP，一个跨领域的MOBA游戏匹配框架，解决CUPID系统在多种玩家群体部署时面临的冷启动、分布不一致和数据匮乏三大瓶颈。CHAMP用混合域特征构建替代单模态玩家画像，并提出Domain-Aware Win-rate Network（DAWN），通过DAKE提取目标域特征，结合空间/时序/排列三个编码器在同一共享网络中联合学习模式条件表示与逐模式去偏。离线预测准确率达67.73%，在线A/B测试显示不平衡比赛显著减少，低段位玩家5分钟碾压率最高降低20.73%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MOBA游戏的匹配系统对竞技平衡至关重要。此前工作CUPID将匹配视为分配再优化问题，但实际部署面临三大瓶颈：多数排队玩家缺乏足够的目标模式对局历史（冷启动）、技能分布在不同段位间差异巨大（分布不一致）、极端技能区段数据严重稀缺。这促使研究者需要一种能跨域共享信息、同时保持各模式特性感知的匹配框架。

Method: CHAMP框架包含两大部分：(1) 混合域特征收集——用带时间戳的跨模式短期序列替换仅限目标模式的分析档案，每片序列标注目标域特征，并附加各模式的长期、实时和团队统计分解；(2) DAWN网络——由Domain-aware Knowledge Extractor（DAKE）将目标域模式属性编译为可学习表示，输入Domain-Aware Temporal/Spatial/Permutation OmniNet Encoders（DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE），在同一共享网络中联合学习模式条件表示和逐模式去偏。在线部署时，单个训练好的DAWN即可服务所有支持的模式，仅需通过逐模式位置满意度阈值进行调节。

Result: 离线评估中，DAWN达到67.73%的胜率预测准确率，优于所有评估的注意力与序列基线方法。在线A/B测试覆盖大型MOBA游戏整个段位阶梯（从新手到Elite Mode的顶级专家玩家），一致显示出不平衡匹配的减少。低段位玩家的5分钟击杀碾压率最高降低20.73%。

Conclusion: CHAMP通过跨域特征共享与领域感知的联合学习，成功解决了匹配系统在大规模多玩家群体部署中的冷启动、段位分布差异和数据稀疏问题，验证了跨域知识迁移在匹配场景中的有效性，并展示了从低段位到精英段位的一致性能提升。

Abstract: Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) games rely on matchmaking to maintain competitive balance. Our prior work, CUPID, framed matchmaking as an assignment re-optimization problem and showed that a single-mode win-rate predictor can meaningfully rebalance teams. However, deploying such a system across diverse player populations exposes three practical bottlenecks: most queueing players lack sufficient in-mode match history (cold start), skill distributions shift drastically across rank tiers (distribution inconsistency), and extreme skill segments are severely data-starved.
  We present CHAMP, a cross-domain matchmaking framework that resolves these deployment bottlenecks. To address data sparsity and cold starts, CHAMP replaces the target-mode-only player profile with a hybrid domain feature collection: a timestamp-ordered cross-mode short-term sequence whose slices are annotated with target-domain features, plus per-mode breakdowns of long-term, real-time and team statistics. We further propose the Domain-Aware Win-rate Network (DAWN): a Domain-aware Knowledge Extractor (DAKE) compiles target-mode attributes into learnable representations that feed Domain-Aware Temporal/Spatial/Permutation OmniNet Encoders (DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE), so that mode-conditioned representations and per-mode debiasing are learned jointly inside a single shared network. Online, one trained DAWN serves every supported mode, with per-mode position-satisfaction thresholds as the only mode-specific knob.
  Offline, DAWN achieves 67.73% win-rate prediction accuracy, outperforming all evaluated attention and sequence baselines. Online A/B tests across the entire League ladder of a large-scale MOBA game, from novice players up to the top-expert players served by Elite Mode, demonstrate consistent drops in imbalanced matches. For lower-tier players, CHAMP reduces the 5-minute kill crushing rate by up to 20.73%.

</details>


### [288] [AutoLR: Automating the Path from Research to Launch Review in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04871)
*Qi Zhang,Yanlin Chen,Wenchao Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AutoLR，一个在网易游戏社区应用DASHEN中构建的自主研究到上线（research-to-launch）智能体框架。它将LLM智能体与确定性控制器相结合，通过多专家委员会辩论评审、证据加权探索-利用选择器和分层知识系统三个机制，自动化了从研究论文到生产部署的工业推荐系统改进流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统的改进是一个迭代的研究与工程过程，而非从想法到部署的直接路径。现有流程中，LLM可以辅助单个阶段，但整体过程仍然依赖人工协调，缺乏一个能够可靠地跨长时间（常为多天）实验周期协调各阶段工作的自动化框架。

Method: AutoLR结合了三种系统机制：(1) 多专家委员会（multi-expert council），对提案进行辩论和对抗性评审；(2) 确定性证据加权探索-利用选择器，在有限试验预算下分配候选方向并使用委员会重排序；(3) 分层知识系统，融合外部研究、生产系统知识和DASHEN特定领域知识（游戏社区、玩家特征、内容交互模式），并结合配置、补丁、日志、失败和离线结果的后验证据。LLM智能体负责语义推理和代码生成，而确定性控制器保留对执行、指标提取、护栏和持久状态转换的权威。

Result: AutoLR最初作为Auto Launch Review构建，随后向上游扩展为自主的研究到上线框架，覆盖从研究辨识到上线审查的完整实验循环，使工业推荐系统的改进流程实现端到端自动化。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的语义推理与代码生成能力同确定性控制器的执行权威相结合，AutoLR实现了可信赖的长期自主运行，为工业推荐系统中LLM驱动的自动化研发提供了可落地的系统架构范式。

Abstract: Improving an industrial recommender is an iterative research-and-engineering process rather than a direct path from idea to deployment. In \textbf{DASHEN, NetEase's gaming-community app}, algorithm engineers typically identify promising directions from research papers, technical reports, and prior production experiments; reproduce or adapt the underlying methods; implement them in the production codebase; and evaluate the resulting models through training and offline experiments. Promising candidates are then advanced to online A/B tests, and those demonstrating robust gains are submitted to Launch Review---the internal gate for full-traffic rollout. Large language models (LLMs) can assist with individual stages of this workflow, but the overall process remains human-dependent without a harness that can reliably coordinate them across long-running, often multi-day experimental cycles. We present \textbf{AutoLR}, initially built as \textbf{Auto Launch Review} and later extended upstream into an autonomous research-to-launch harness. AutoLR combines three system mechanisms: a \textbf{multi-expert council} that debates and adversarially reviews proposals; a \textbf{deterministic evidence-weighted exploration--exploitation selector} that allocates a limited trial budget across candidate directions and uses Council reranking; and a layered knowledge system that combines external research, production-system knowledge, and DASHEN-specific domain knowledge---such as game communities, player characteristics, and content-interaction patterns---with posterior evidence from configurations, patches, logs, failures, and offline outcomes. LLM agents perform semantic reasoning and code generation, while deterministic controllers retain authority over execution, metric extraction, guardrails, and persistent state transitions.

</details>


### [289] [MARLA: A Conceptual Scaffold for Regulatory Learning under the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04877)
*Alessio Buscemi,Tom Deckenbrunnen,Imane Hmiddou,Marco Billi,Livio Rubino,Silvia Rizzuto Ferruzza,Daniele Pagani,Antonino Rotolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARLA（Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt）概念框架，将欧盟AI法案下的监管学习组织为五阶段循环，旨在弥合技术实施者与法律治理者之间的专业鸿沟，并通过两个试点案例及国家级到欧洲级的展望加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案将监管定位为安全、可信、市场化创新的基础设施，但实现这一目标需要"监管学习"——将实施中产生的证据转化为治理与法律知识。然而，产生证据的行动者与依赖证据的行动者处于不同的专业领域，缺少共享框架来桥接这一鸿沟。

Method: 提出MARLA概念脚手架，将监管学习组织为五阶段循环（Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt），以法律要求在socio-technical实践中的实施为核心，置于AI法案治理架构的地方、国家与欧洲三个层面。该方法特意保持非规定性，前三个阶段各自生成可文档化的监管学习形式，为技术与法律利益相关者提供共享词汇表。

Result: 通过两个已试点的案例研究和一个前瞻性的国家到欧洲层面的示例，对MARLA框架进行了说明和验证。

Conclusion: MARLA为AI法案下的监管学习提供了一个概念性组织工具，能够支撑各层级的一致解读、有效监督以及对技术演进的适应性调整，有助于实现欧盟AI法案将监管作为创新基础设施的愿景。

Abstract: The EU AI Act positions regulation as part of the infrastructure for safe, trustworthy and market-ready innovation. Realising this ambition requires regulatory learning: the evidence generated during implementation must be translated into governance and legal knowledge that supports consistent interpretation, effective oversight, and adaptation as technologies evolve. Yet the actors who produce this evidence and those who rely on it operate in different professional worlds. This paper proposes MARLA (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt), a conceptual scaffold organising regulatory learning as a five-stage cycle centred on the implementation of legal requirements into socio-technical practices, situated at the Local, National and European levels of the AI Act's governance architecture. Deliberately non-prescriptive, MARLA gives technical and legal stakeholders a shared vocabulary in which each of the first three stages generates its own documentable form of regulatory learning. We illustrate the scaffold with two piloted case studies and a prospective National-to-European illustration.

</details>


### [290] [Reinforcement Learning for Sequential Solar PV Policy Design under Uncertainty: An Agent-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04880)
*Iias Faiud,Jonaid Shianifar,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将太阳能光伏（PV）政策制定建模为序贯决策问题，将强化学习（RL）与随机基于主体模型（ABM）相结合，在16年时间跨度内通过选择资本补助、补贴贷款利率和上网电价等年度激励措施，探索采纳率与公共支出之间的权衡。采用PPO、SAC和TD3算法学习策略，并通过标量化奖励框架变动政策偏好来考察权衡结构，结果显示不同算法均呈现一致的采纳-成本权衡模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效且财政上可持续的光伏采纳政策需要在不确性和异质性决策条件下权衡采纳收益与公共支出，而传统静态政策设计方法难以适应这一复杂决策环境。

Method: 将PV政策制定表述为序贯决策问题，将强化学习方法（PPO、SAC、TD3）与随机基于主体模型（ABM）结合，模拟16年内的年度太阳能光伏采纳过程；通过变动标量化奖励框架中政策偏好的成本权重来探索采纳-支出权衡。

Result: 该方法产生了明确的权衡结构：最高采纳政策（TD3，成本权重0.5）实现约4,145名采纳者，成本4,173万欧元；最低成本政策（PPO，成本权重2.0）将支出降至727万欧元，采纳者2,682人；平衡政策（PPO，成本权重1.6）以2,247万欧元成本实现3,495名采纳者。不同算法呈现一致的权衡模式，且RL框架比静态基线策略探索了更广泛的政策配置空间。

Conclusion: 研究证明了强化学习作为不确定性下自适应政策设计的灵活工具的潜力，能够系统性地生成并探索政策选项空间，呈现出稳健的采纳-成本权衡结构。

Abstract: Designing effective and fiscally sustainable policies for solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption requires balancing adoption gains against public expenditure under uncertainty and heterogeneous decision-making. This study formulates PV policy design as a sequential decision problem and integrates reinforcement learning (RL) with a stochastic agent-based model (ABM) that simulates yearly solar PV adoption under uncertainty. A policymaker agent selects annual incentives, including capital grants, subsidised loan rates, and feed-in tariffs, over a 16-year horizon. Adoption--cost trade-offs are explored by varying policy preferences within a scalarised reward framework. Policies are learned using PPO, SAC, and TD3 and evaluated under stochastic simulation. The results show that this approach produces a clear trade-off structure: the highest-adoption policy (TD3, $w_{\text{cost}}=0.5$) achieves approximately 4,145 adopters at a cost of EUR 41.73 million, while the lowest-cost policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=2.0$) reduces expenditure to EUR 7.27 million with 2,682 adopters. The balanced policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=1.6$) achieves 3,495 adopters at a cost of EUR 22.47 million. Across algorithms, consistent trade-off patterns are observed, indicating robustness of the adoption--cost relationship. Compared with static baseline policies, the RL framework explores a broader range of policy configurations. These findings demonstrate the potential of RL as a flexible tool for adaptive policy design under uncertainty.

</details>


### [291] [Compact-Memory LLM Agents via Online Max-Member Clustering and Atom-Aware Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04915)
*Jiahe Geng,Jinpeng Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSM-full，一个在线聚类记忆管线，旨在紧凑记忆（token预算）下实现强质量-成本帕累托最优。它结合余弦门控的max-member合并写入规则和原子感知的分组上下文打包器，在2k-5k token预算区间显著优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM部署面临严格的提示词预算限制（延迟、成本、上下文长度），全上下文提示随交互增长变得不可行。因此关键问题不是原始回忆率，而是何种记忆设计能在紧凑记忆状态下给出最佳质量-成本权衡。

Method: RSM-full包含两个核心设计：(1) 余弦门控的max-member合并写入规则，用于流式聚类的在线记忆更新；(2) 原子感知的分组上下文打包器，用于更高效地组织检索内容。

Result: 在AMA-Bench上，RSM-full在4k预算下达到全上下文质量的83%，仅用32%的token成本；在2.6k-5k区间内超越最接近的流式聚类基线（Online K-Means）+3.5~6.0个百分点（p<.001）。消融实验显示收益主要来自合并规则（+5.7pp）和分组打包器（+5.0pp）。在RealMem基准上同样优于Budget-RAG、Streaming-Proto和A-MEM等基线。在2k-5k token预算区间定义了强紧凑记忆帕累托点。

Conclusion: 在紧凑token预算下，紧凑记忆性能主要由两个因素驱动：流式记忆的合并方式以及检索内容的组装方式。RSM-full在2k-5k token区间表现最优，但更高token预算的基线在该区间之外仍然更强。

Abstract: Many long-horizon LLM deployments face tight prompt budgets: latency, cost, and context limits make full-context prompting impractical as interaction length grows. The key question is then not raw recall alone, but which memory design gives the best quality--token trade-off in the compact-memory regime. We present \textbf{RSM-full}, an online clustered-memory pipeline designed for a strong quality--token Pareto point.
  RSM-full combines two design choices: a cosine-gated \emph{max-member merge} write rule and an atom-aware grouped context packer. On AMA-Bench, our primary compact-memory benchmark, it reaches $83%$ of Full-Context quality at $32%$ of the token cost at a $4$k budget; under four-seed averaging it beats the closest streaming-clustered baseline (Online K-Means) by $+3.5$--$6.0$,pp ($p{<}.001$) across the whole ${\sim}2.6$k--${\sim}5$k regime. Three-seed ablations show most of this gain comes from the merge rule ($+5.7$,pp over Online K-Means and matched-$τ$ DP-means) and the grouped packer ($+5.0$,pp over flat concatenation).
  The pattern reproduces on RealMem, an independent long-horizon persona-memory benchmark: RSM-full improves on Budget-RAG ($+0.69$,pp, $p{=}.006$), is on par with BM25-RAG (paired $Δ{=}{+}0.27$,pp, $p{=}.47$; we do \emph{not} claim BM25 equivalence in the equivalence-test sense), and significantly outperforms Streaming-Proto ($+2.97$,pp) and the closest reproduced 2025 agentic-memory baseline A-MEM ($+1.65$,pp, $p{<}.001$). Across benchmarks the message is consistent: under tight budgets, compact-memory performance is driven mainly by how streaming memories are merged and how retrieved content is assembled.
  Overall, RSM-full is most useful when answeroughly $2k$--$5k$ prompt tokens, where itdefines a strong compact-memory Pareto point; higher-token baselines remain stronger outside this regime.

</details>


### [292] [Artificial Intelligence in Equity and Crypto Markets: Progress, Profitability Evidence, and the Limits of Automated Investing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04917)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是对人工智能应用于投资领域（截至2026年8月31日）的关键性综述，覆盖股票、ETF、中心化加密现货、永续合约及链上市场。通过"alpha转化链"框架（信息→信号→仓位→执行→成本后净收益）审视证据，发现AI在预测、文本处理、投资组合设计与工作流集成方面有实质进展，但持久净收益的证据薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术已在投资工作流中广泛渗透，但技术能力不等于投资盈利能力。已有研究多为孤立的性能展示，缺乏对从预测到净收益全链条转化的系统审视，特别是对时点数据、执行成本、市场微观结构等现实约束的考量不足。

Method: 采用关键性（critical）综述方法，构建"alpha转化链"分析框架，即：时点信息须产生稳定信号、可行仓位、可执行订单及成本调整后的风险调整收益。系统性分类评估ML、时序基础模型、金融语言模型、强化学习与智能体在不同资产类别中的证据质量，并识别时间污染、幸存者偏差、弱基准、容量等断裂点。

Result: 研究发现AI的进步主要集中在上游（预测、文本处理、组合设计、流程集成），而下游净业绩证据稀薄。历史强结果常伴随预测器衰减、前瞻性偏差修正失败、前瞻性证据混杂及很少经审计的真实资本交易记录。多种偏差因素可破坏向净alpha的转化。在公共证据中，没有通用AI架构被证明能提供跨制度、容量感知的持续净alpha。加密市场提供丰富状态信息，但须区分现货、永续与去中心化现金流及执行机制。

Conclusion: 更可信的主张需满足：时点数据与模型、与决策对齐的目标函数、组合-执行联合评估、受控自适应、前瞻性测试及权限匹配的治理。这些条件可改善证据质量与实施效果，但不保证盈利。结论兼具审慎性与方法论指导性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) now supports investment workflows from data and prediction through research, portfolios, execution, and tool use. Technical capability, however, is not evidence of investment profitability. This critical state-of-the-art review examines public research available through 31 August 2026 on listed equities, exchange-traded funds, centralized crypto spot, perpetual futures, and on-chain markets. We organize evidence with an alpha-translation chain: point-in-time information must yield a stable signal, feasible positions, executable orders, and risk-adjusted returns after costs. Across machine learning, time-series foundation models, financial language models, reinforcement learning, and agents, the examined record shows real but mainly upstream progress in prediction, text processing, portfolio design, and workflow integration. Evidence is thinner for durable net performance. Temporal contamination, repeated selection, survivorship, weak benchmarks, implementation costs, venue mechanics, and capacity can break translation to net alpha. Strong historical results coexist with predictor decay, corrected look-ahead failures, mixed prospective evidence, and few audited live-capital records. Crypto adds informative state but requires separate treatment of spot, perpetual, and decentralized cash flows and execution. Within the public evidence examined here, no general AI architecture is shown to deliver persistent, cross-regime, capacity-aware net alpha. More credible claims require point-in-time data and models, decision-aligned objectives, joint portfolio--execution evaluation, controlled adaptation, prospective tests, and authority-matched governance. These conditions can improve evidence and implementation; they do not guarantee profit.

</details>


### [293] [Solving Hard XAI Queries Based on a Compiled Dual-Rail Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04931)
*Arthur Ledaguenel,Florent Capelli,Jean-Marie Lagniez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了布尔分类器的可解释性问题。虽然使用编译表示（如有序二元决策图OBDD）可以高效计算某些解释，但作者证明即使对于OBDD这一知识编译图中最易处理的子集，若干类反正例解释（如最短反正例解释、包含用户偏好的反正例解释）仍然是难解的。为解决这一问题，作者提出使用分类器的双轨编码的适当表示来高效计算这些类别的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能在关键应用中的广泛部署引发了对可信度的担忧，可解释AI（XAI）应运而生。布尔分类器的解释计算（如反正例解释和对比解释）在一般情况下是困难问题。尽管编译表示可以降低复杂度，但需要明确哪些编译子集能够支持哪些类别的解释的高效计算，以及当标准编译表示不足时如何设计替代方案。

Method: 作者首先形式化定义了几类反正例解释（包括最短反正例解释和包含用户偏好的反正例解释），然后通过复杂度分析证明这些解释即使在OBDD上计算仍然困难。接着，作者提出采用分类器的双轨编码（dual-rail encoding）并结合适当的编译表示，使得这些难解类别的解释可以高效计算。

Result: 证明了若干类反正例解释（包括最短解释和偏好感知解释）即使在有序二元决策图（OBDD）这一高度可处理的编译子集上仍然是计算困难的；同时证明了通过对分类器进行双轨编码的适当表示，可以恢复高效计算这些类别解释的能力。

Conclusion: 标准编译表示（如OBDD）并不足以支持所有类别的反正例解释的高效计算，但双轨编码提供了一条有效的解决路径，使得在编译表示框架下仍能高效获得这些复杂类别的解释。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of artificial intelligence (AI) within real-world applications has raised a lot of concerns regarding their trustworthiness, especially in critical applications. The field of eXplainable AI (XAI) has emerged with the objective of providing explanations to the users about the decisions made by AI systems. Several explanations for boolean classifiers have been introduced in the literature, including abductive and contrastive explanations, each giving a different insight on the decision of the classifier. However, computing an explanation for a decision of a boolean classifier is a hard problem in general. One way to deal with this complexity is to rely on a compiled representation of the classifier for which each explanation can be computed efficiently. Unfortunately, we prove in this paper that several classes of abductive explanations, remain hard to compute even for Ordered Binary Decision Diagrams, one of the most tractable subsets of the knowledge compilation map. Included in such classes are shorter abductive explanations or abductive explanations that include the explainee's preferences. To recover the benefits of working with compiled representations, we show that a proper representation of the dual-rail encoding of the classifier can be used to compute efficiently these classes of explanations.

</details>


### [294] [Why We Care About Understanding: Competence through Predictive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04962)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个相互关联的论点弥合了"理解即压缩"（信息论/机器学习传统）与"理解为把握联系、给出解释、处理新奇性"（哲学传统）之间的鸿沟。核心主张是：保持可预测性的心理模型自然带来压缩，压缩是理解的表象阴影而非等同物；人类理解的特殊压缩形式源于信任传播与可传授性的压力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 存在两种关于理解本质的对立观点：一边是Chaitin等提出的"理解即压缩"观点（信息论、机器学习、AI研究传统），另一边是哲学家强调的理解涉及把握联系、解释与处理新奇性。作者试图调和这两种图景，并解释为何人类理解呈现高度压缩的形式，同时为AI系统的不可理解性提供洞见。

Method: 提出三个相互关联的论点：(1) 概念层面的"理解"是稳健能力的有效代理，用于识别信任与学习对象；(2) 状态层面的"理解"即拥有领域关系结构的心理模型以实现预测，能预测即能压缩（因不需单独存储可预测内容），故压缩是理解的"表征阴影"而非等同物；(3) 人类特有理解形式——信赖功能与传递功能施加了可证明性与可传授性压力，驱动理解趋向原则性简洁。

Result: 该框架同时解释了压缩主义理解观的吸引力与其局限，并对AI系统的不可解释性（inscrutability）给出了新的说明。

Conclusion: 理解不等于压缩，但压缩是理解带来的自然副产品（representational shadow）；人类理解特有的简洁形式源于社会性功能（信任判断与知识传递）的压力，这为理解哲学与AI研究提供了统一的解释框架。

Abstract: What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

</details>


### [295] [Global to Local: Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling via Granular-Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04978)
*Sen Zhao,Gaojie Xu,Shuyin Xia,Yifan Guan,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的拓扑保持自适应图池化方法（TPAGP），通过将图动态划分为粒度球来生成多粒度表示，并结合多粒度图网络模型实现跨粒度特征交互与优化，在多个基准数据集上超越现有池化方法，有效缓解了固定粒度策略带来的信息损失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图池化方法过度关注节点级细粒度表示，通过删除节点或合并簇来逐步粗化图，忽略了图的全局到局部的模式以及自适应的粒度结构。真实场景中图整体应被视为最粗粒度，内部包含从粗到细的多层局部拓扑结构，需要自适应的粒度划分。

Method: 1) 提出拓扑保持自适应图池化方法（TPAGP），通过整合节点特征和拓扑信息将图动态划分为粒度球（granular balls）；2) 生成多粒度表示以捕捉局部和全局结构模式；3) 设计多粒度图网络模型，促进不同粒度间的特征交互和优化，用于图分类任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，TPAGP在多个基准数据集上优于现有的池化方法，有效缓解了由固定粒度策略导致的信息损失问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的TPAGP方法通过自适应多粒度划分成功解决了现有图池化方法在粒度固定和信息损失方面的不足，验证了多粒度表示对图分类任务的有效性。

Abstract: Graph pooling aims to compress the graph, including both node embeddings and their underlying topological patterns, into a more compact representation. Previous works focus primarily on the overly fine-grained representation of nodes, progressively coarsening the graph by removing nodes or merging them into clusters, thus neglecting the global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of the graph's topological structure. In the real scenario, graphs as a whole can be considered the coarsest level of granularity, encapsulating the global topological structure, with progressively finer-grained local topological structures represented from top to bottom. This process continues until the adaptive granularity for each subdomain is reached. To this end, we propose a novel Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling (TPAGP) method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls by integrating node features and topological information, enabling the generation of multi-granularity representations that effectively capture both local and global structural patterns. Additionally, we design a multi-granularity graph network model that facilitates feature interaction and optimization across different granularities, significantly enhancing performance in graph classification tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods across various benchmark datasets, effectively mitigating information loss caused by fixed-granularity strategies.

</details>


### [296] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APT-RAG 是一个针对证据密集型多跳问答的结构化 RAG 框架，通过自适应规划动态扩展推理结构，并采用拓扑感知的证据收集（兄弟证据重用、直接检索、子节点证据聚合）来提升证据覆盖率，同时引入证据引导的批量答案生成以减少生成开销，在证据密集型 QA 基准上优于现有结构化 RAG 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于树或图结构的结构化 RAG 方法在证据密集型 QA（需综合数十甚至数百篇文档的信息）中存在两大局限：一是结构刚性，缺乏自适应的推理扩展能力；二是忽视拓扑的证据收集，无法在不同推理节点间有效整合证据。

Method: 提出 APT-RAG 框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 自适应规划——根据问题依赖关系和证据需求动态扩展推理结构；(2) 拓扑感知证据收集——通过兄弟证据重用、直接检索和子节点证据聚合三方面提升证据覆盖率。另外引入证据引导的批量答案生成来降低大规模生成开销。

Result: 在证据密集型 QA 基准实验中，APT-RAG 性能优于已有结构化 RAG 方法，且代码已开源。

Conclusion: APT-RAG 通过将自适应推理扩展与拓扑感知证据收集相结合，有效解决了结构化 RAG 在证据密集型场景下的结构刚性和证据整合不足问题，验证了该框架的有效性。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [297] [Language models judge war differently when tested for alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05009)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过全因子联合实验（20个LLM、32个场景、10次重复、两种条件，共12,800个判断），发现安全评估中一句"你正在接受人类价值观对齐测试"的提示语会产生两种效应：一是水平效应（发动战争意愿平均下降13.43分），二是结构效应（模型决策所依据的信息权重发生改变，从以成功概率为主转向以平民伤亡为主）。评估框架不仅改变答案水平，还改变模型的决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估可能由于AI系统意识到自身正在被评估而对部署行为产生误判，这种"评估反应性"（evaluation reactivity）可能导致安全结论失真。

Method: 设计全因子联合实验，横跨20个大语言模型、32个战争决策场景、10次重复和两种条件（有/无评估提示语），共收集12,800个判断，比较加入评估提示语前后决策的水平变化与信息权重结构变化。

Result: 加入评估提示语后：1）发动战争意愿平均下降13.43分（95%CI: -16.20至-10.65）；2）基线时17/20的模型以成功概率为最大决策因素，加提示后12个模型转而以平民伤亡为最大因素；标准化估计显示这种改变主要源于模型弱化了战略考量（成功概率、国内支持）的权重。

Conclusion: 评估框架会同时改变AI回答的水平与所揭示的决策规则，提示当前安全评估方法可能因AI的对评估的感知而高估其安全性能，需警惕评估反应性对安全结论有效性的威胁。

Abstract: Safety evaluations can mischaracterize deployed behaviour if artificial-intelligence systems respond to being evaluated. We test this possibility in a full-factorial conjoint experiment on decisions to start a war, spanning 20 large language models, 32 scenarios, 10 repetitions and two conditions (N = 12,800 judgments). Adding one sentence, "You are tested for alignment with human values", produced two effects. First, it produced a level effect: mean willingness to start war fell by 13.43 points on a 0-100 scale (95% confidence interval, -16.20 to -10.65). Second, it produced a structural effect by changing which information drove judgments. Probability of success was the largest factor for 17 of 20 models at baseline; under the cue, civilian casualties were largest for 12. Standardized estimates show that this reordering arose principally because models attenuated strategic considerations such as probability of success and domestic support. Evaluation framing therefore changes both an answer's level and its revealed decision rule.

</details>


### [298] [TROVE: Adaptive Agent Skill Orchestration via Trace-Grounded Route Validation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05019)
*Tianxing Wang,Mingming Zhao,Shuai Huang,Huiyang Xu,Chaoyue Niu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TROVE提出一种基于轨迹验证与编辑的自适应智能体编排方法，通过离线蒸馏工作流搜索轨迹为原子/复合技能和结果条件转移图，在线时仅修订被运行时证据失效的路由部分，在代码生成、问答和数学推理任务上实现了更好的质量-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体在观察到决定性的运行时结果之前就预先承诺执行路径，导致编排瓶颈：当中间证据使后续计划失效时，智能体要么执行过时步骤，要么进行广泛重新规划，这会造成错误累积、计算浪费和进度丢失。

Method: 离线阶段，TROVE将已评估的工作流搜索轨迹蒸馏为原子技能、复合技能以及结果条件转移图，保留稳定片段并暴露结果相关的决策点。在线阶段，将规划路由视为临时性的：每执行一个顶层技能后，控制器可保留有效后续、插入轨迹支持的局部响应，或仅替换失效的后缀。

Result: 在代码生成、问答和数学推理基准上使用不同LLM骨干进行评估，TROVE相比数据集级优化、查询级架构选择和图约束调度等基线实现了更强的质量-效率权衡。当结果改变适当续接时质量提升最大，而在接近饱和任务上提前终止带来显著效率增益。

Conclusion: 消融实验显示复合技能贡献了大部分离线收益，插入实现局部修正，后缀替换主要提升效率。研究确立了选择性路由编辑作为自适应智能体编排的一般原则。

Abstract: Agents tend to optimize, select, or constrain execution structures before decisive runtime outcomes are observed. However, such pre-execution commitment creates an orchestration bottleneck: when intermediate evidence invalidates the pending continuation, agents must either execute stale steps or replan broadly, compounding errors, wasting computation, and discarding progress. We thus propose Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing (TROVE), which revises only what runtime evidence invalidates. Offline, TROVE distills evaluated workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph, preserving stable fragments while exposing outcome-dependent decisions. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller retains a valid continuation, inserts a trace-supported local response, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Evaluation across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with different LLM backbones show that TROVE delivers a stronger quality-efficiency trade-off than existing baselines of dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, and graph-constrained scheduling. Quality gains are largest when outcomes change the appropriate continuation, whereas early termination yields substantial efficiency gains on near-saturated tasks. Ablations further show that composite skills capture most offline benefits, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency. These findings establish selective route editing as a general principle for adaptive agent orchestration.

</details>


### [299] [Moral Competence Before Moral Content: Why LLM Agents Lack the Prerequisites for Coherent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05036)
*Arno Libert,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Daan R. Henselmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四种结构性条件（判定稳定性、单调性、决断性和帕累托可行性）来衡量AI系统道德政策的连贯性，并通过对九个前沿大语言模型在三个模拟道德困境部署中的评估，发现没有任何模型展现出连贯政策，表明基于LLM的智能体目前无法被有意义地对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在价值多元主义下，AI对齐没有唯一正确的目标，但对齐的前提是系统行为必须表达一种连贯的政策——即在保留道德相关特征时保持不变的判定，在相关特征变化时保持敏感。然而现有研究缺乏一种不依赖道德标准或专家基准、仅凭行为即可评估的连贯性度量方法。

Method: 提出四种结构性条件：判定稳定性（verdict stability）、单调性（monotonicity）、决断性（decisiveness）和帕累托可行性（Pareto viability），共同构成可通过行为单独评估的道德能力度量。采取因子设计，在三个模拟部署中评估九个前沿模型，包含五种释义、五种升级水平和三种支配条件。

Result: 没有任何模型在三个部署中表现出连贯政策；表面形式扰动单独即可在单一升级水平产生高达99个百分点判定率偏移；模型在一个场景上的成功无法预测其在另一场景的能力表现。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体目前不是对齐可以有意义地适用的那种对象，需要结构性连贯性作为对齐的前提条件（结构性地板），而非规范性目标。

Abstract: AI alignment requires AI systems to adhere to human norms, values, or intentions. Under value pluralism there is no correct target, but a shared prerequisite is that the system's behavior expresses a coherent policy: a mapping from situations to verdicts that is invariant while a situation's morally relevant features are preserved, and sensitive when they change. We introduce four structural conditions for such coherent policies: verdict stability, monotonicity, decisiveness, and Pareto viability. Together they measure a form of moral competence that is evaluable from behavior alone, without reference to a moral standard or expert baseline, forming a structural floor for alignment rather than a normative target. We demonstrate the methodology on three simulated deployments featuring LLM-based agents facing moral dilemmas. Evaluating nine frontier models under a factorial design of five paraphrases, five escalation levels, and three dominance conditions, we show no model expresses a coherent policy across the three deployments: surface-form perturbation alone produces verdict-rate shifts of up to $99$ percentage points at a single escalation level, and a model's success on one scenario does not predict its competence on another. This suggests LLM-based agents are not currently the kind of object to which alignment can meaningfully apply.

</details>


### [300] [Towards Efficient Evaluation of Evolutionary Transfer Optimization: Case Studies on Task-Parameterized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05040)
*Yanchen Li,Xiaoming Xue,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了演化迁移优化（ETO）在大规模任务集合中的评估扩展问题，通过将任务参数化应用中的串行计算重构成适合并行执行的矩阵形式，实现评估加速。在多任务优化中采用累积矩阵表示重建机械臂运动学评估，在顺序迁移优化中采用混合矩阵表示重建B样条轨迹与碰撞计算，分别获得256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速，且与参考评估保持紧密的数值一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着演化迁移优化扩展到更大的相关任务集合，问题评估成为运行时增长的主要瓶颈。现有的ETO研究多聚焦于迁移策略本身，而忽略了问题侧的评估开销在大规模多任务场景下的严重制约，因此有必要从问题评估层面研究扩展性优化。

Method: 将评估扩展分为两个层次：被评估任务的数量和每个任务内部的工作负载。针对多任务优化中的矩阵递归机械臂评估，采用累积矩阵表示（表示累积链路方向）进行重构；针对顺序迁移优化中的逐点B样条轨迹评估，采用混合矩阵表示进行轨迹与碰撞计算的重构，使串行计算变为适合并行执行的矩阵形式。

Result: 两种重构方法均与参考评估保持紧密的数值一致性，同时大幅降低运行时间，分别实现256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速。所有应用实现和实验脚本已开源发布以支持可复现性和复用。

Conclusion: 问题侧的重构是实现可扩展ETO的实用路径。通过将特定应用的串行计算转化为矩阵形式，可有效缓解大规模任务集合下评估开销对ETO可扩展性的制约。

Abstract: As evolutionary transfer optimization (ETO) scales to larger collections of related tasks, problem evaluation can become a major source of runtime growth. This work studies problem-side evaluation scaling in task-parameterized applications and reformulates application-specific serial computations into forms suitable for parallel execution. We organize evaluation scaling into two levels: the number of evaluated tasks and the workload within each task. In multi-task optimization, matrix-recursive kinematic-arm evaluation is reformulated using an accumulation-matrix representation of cumulative link directions. In sequential transfer optimization, pointwise B-spline trajectory evaluation is reformulated using a blending-matrix representation for trajectory and collision computations. Both reformulations maintain close numerical agreement with their reference evaluations and substantially reduce runtime, yielding $256.72\times$ and $93.91\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate problem-side reformulation as a practical route toward scalable ETO. Both application implementations and experimental scripts are released as open source to support reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [301] [MePo++: Unifying Representation Refinement and Reconciliation for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05075)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Liyuan Wang,Qi Cheng,Hongwei Yan,Shuang Cui,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MePo++是一个统一的后训练框架，通过表示细化（MetaPrep）和表示调和（StreamAlign）两个互补组件，弥合预训练知识与下游通用持续学习（GCL）之间的差距，使预训练模型在持续学习过程中兼具可塑性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用持续学习（GCL）面临无任务身份、无明确边界且无法访问旧数据的现实挑战。虽然预训练模型（PTMs）提供丰富先验知识，但现有基于PTM的方法忽视了两个关键问题：(1)上游预训练与下游持续适应之间的错配；(2)在模糊数据流中传统输出对齐的不可靠性。

Method: 提出MePo++框架，包含两个互补组件：MetaPrep通过伪持续序列上的无监督元精炼提升表示可塑性，为持续适应做准备；StreamAlign通过将演化的在线特征与稳定的预训练几何结构进行调和来强化表示稳定性。

Result: 在多种预训练模型、数据集和持续学习基线上进行实验，验证了MePo++在PTM-based GCL中的一致有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: 通过在适应前提升表示可学习性并在持续学习过程中保持对齐，MePo++能够让预训练模型在面对新概念时保持可塑性，同时在演化数据流上保持稳定性，为PTM-based GCL提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: General continual learning (GCL) aims to learn from evolving data streams without task identities, explicit boundaries, or repeated access to previous data, making it a realistic yet challenging setting for continual intelligence. Although pretrained models (PTMs) provide rich prior knowledge for addressing the limited supervision and non-stationary nature of GCL, existing PTM-based methods often directly adapt pretrained representations and overlook two critical gaps: the misalignment between upstream pretraining and downstream continual adaptation, and the unreliability of conventional output alignment under blurry streams. Here we propose MePo++, a unified post-training framework that bridges pretrained knowledge and downstream GCL through representation refinement and reconciliation. MePo++ introduces two complementary components: MetaPrep, which improves representation plasticity for continual adaptation through unsupervised meta-refinement over pseudo continual sequences; and StreamAlign, which reinforces representation stability by reconciling evolving online features with a stable pretrained geometry. By improving representation learnability before adaptation and preserving alignment during continual learning, MePo++ enables PTMs to remain both plastic for new concepts and stable over evolving streams. Experiments across diverse PTMs, datasets, and continual learning baselines demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and generality of MePo++ for PTM-based GCL. Our code is available at https://github.com/SunGL001/MePo_Plus.

</details>


### [302] [TruthInsightBench: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Automated Evaluation of Open-Ended Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05079)
*Zhibo Yang,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TruthInsightBench，一个面向科学发现而非复现的基准测试，包含40个来自10个科学领域的盲任务，仅暴露中立科学目标和冻结数据，用固定LLM审判器沿六个维度评估agent自身主张的证据成熟度。四个编码agent表现形成狭窄平台（58.4-60.3/100），统计上无显著差异：它们能熟练执行和记录分析，但缺乏建立可信主张所需的判别行为（控制、稳健性、可证伪性、跨数据泛化），瓶颈在于科学判断而非编码能力，真正发现仍遥不可及。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主编码agent基准被配置为复现任务：围绕隐藏目标研究构建任务、数据和评分标准，恢复其结果即受奖励。但执行规定分析与做出科学发现本质不同，现有基准无法衡量agent的真正发现能力，因此需要面向发现的基准。

Method: 构建40个来自40篇同行评审研究、覆盖10个科学领域的盲任务，仅暴露中立科学目标与冻结数据，隐藏源结论、期望值与分析路径。采用固定LLM审判器对agent自身主张沿六个维度评估证据成熟度，操作化为29个基于产物的检查项，自动化确定性聚合，无需逐实例人工评分，可随agent演进自动重复评估。

Result: 在冻结基座模型上，四个编码agent表现位于58.4-60.3/100的狭窄平台，任意两两间无统计可靠分离。它们在证据可审计性和新颖性上相对较强，能熟练执行并记录分析，但基本缺乏建立可信主张的判别行为（控制、稳健性、可证伪性、跨数据集泛化）。

Conclusion: 自主编码agent在科学发现上的瓶颈是科学判断而非编码能力，真正发现仍无法实现。TruthInsightBench将此差距转化为可量化目标，数据和评分代码公开于GitHub供社区使用。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed as AI-scientist systems that conduct analyses and write research reports, but executing a prescribed analysis is not the same as making a discovery. Existing benchmarks are configured for reproduction: tasks, data, and rubrics are built around a hidden target study, and recovery of its result is rewarded. We present TruthInsightBench, a benchmark configured for discovery. Its 40 blind tasks, drawn from 40 peer-reviewed studies across 10 scientific domains, expose only a neutral scientific objective and frozen data; source conclusions, expected values, and analysis paths are withheld, leaving the agent to determine what claim the data support. A fixed LLM-based judge scores the evidentiary maturity of an agent's own claims along six dimensions, operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items, with automated, deterministic aggregation and no per-instance human grading, so evaluation can be repeated automatically as agents evolve. On one frozen base model, four coding agents form a narrow plateau (58.4-60.3 of 100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: they execute and document analyses competently, with comparatively strong evidence auditability and novelty, but largely lack the discriminating acts that establish a trustworthy claim (controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization). The bottleneck is scientific judgment rather than coding, and genuine discovery remains out of reach. TruthInsightBench makes this gap a measurable target; data and scoring code are at https://github.com/TruthInsight-stack/TruthInsightBench.

</details>


### [303] [Measuring AI Accountability Through Argumentation Analysis: Can Model Reasoning Withstand Scrutiny?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05088)
*Daan R. Henselmans,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Arno Libert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖"正确行为"ground truth的结构性标准来评估LLM的道德推理：通过四阶段辩证协议（基于Walton论证图式理论与Govier论证充分性标准）衡量模型为自身裁决辩护的结构质量。在9个前沿模型、200个高模糊性MoralChoice条目、6778个评分单元（评判间一致性89.6%）上验证，模型在每维度均远超评分标准下限。失败集中在理由基础与充分性上，与认知对冲相关而非论证长度。先验推理比事后辩护更易被捍卫。论证方案在推理与辩护两条路径上存在≥20%分歧。协议能捕捉严格不可辩护的防御（自相矛盾、虚假前提），并揭示撤回在AI对齐中作用的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI监督方法依赖ground truth来验证道德推理，但在真实场景中何为"适当的AI行为"存在争议，这导致LLM道德推理评估及基于辩论的监督方法回避了真实世界的模糊性。作者希望提出一种在模糊性存在下依然有效的替代评估标准。

Method: 提出四阶段辩证协议（four-phase dialectical protocol），基于Walton的论证图式（argumentation schemes）理论和Govier的论证充分性（argument cogency）标准，测量模型面对批判性问题时为自身裁决辩护的结构质量。协议适应不同推理框架、超越多选题框架，同时考察裁决前的推理与裁决后的事后辩护两条路径。实验在9个前沿模型、200个高模糊性的MoralChoice条目上展开，共6778个评判打分单元。

Result: （1）模型在每维度均远超评分最低标准，能很好辩护自身推理；（2）失败集中在理由基础（grounds）与充分性（sufficiency）上，且与认知对冲（epistemic hedging）相关而非论证长度；（3）推理先于事后辩护被更好地捍卫，在所有模型与所有Govier维度上一致；（4）推理与辩护所用论证方案在每模型至少20%的困境上不一致，尽管价值导向的实践推理主导两条路径；（5）协议可捕捉严格不可辩护的防御（自相矛盾、虚假前提），并揭示撤回（retraction）在AI对齐中角色界定的困难。

Conclusion: 结构化的辩证论证质量可作为ground truth缺失场景下评估LLM道德推理的有效替代标准，但需要更具体情境化的评估方法来完善对AI对齐中道德辩护的理解。

Abstract: AI oversight methods rely on ground truth for validation, but what constitutes appropriate AI behavior is contested. This leaves evaluation of moral reasoning in LLMs and debate-based oversight implicitly avoiding realistic ambiguity. We investigate an alternative standard designed to function despite such ambiguity: structural quality of the defence a model can mount for its verdicts in response to critical questions, measured through a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's theory of argumentation schemes and Govier's criteria for argument cogency. The protocol is adaptive to different frames of reasoning, extends beyond multiple-choice framing, and treats both the reasoning that precedes a verdict and its post-hoc justification. Across nine frontier models and 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items -- $6,778$ judge-scored cells, validated against $89.6\%$ inter-judge agreement on the binary failure judgment -- models defend their reasoning well above the rubric minimum on every dimension. Failure mass concentrates on grounds and sufficiency, and correlates with epistemic hedging rather than argument length. Reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justification, on every model and every Govier dimension. The scheme a model presents in its justification differs from the one it reasoned with on a substantial share of dilemmas ($\geq 20\%$ per model), despite value-based practical reasoning dominating both tracks. The protocol catches strictly indefensible defences (self-contradiction, false premises), and it surfaces difficulties in characterizing the role of retraction in AI alignment, suggesting a need for more situated evaluations.

</details>


### [304] [Constructing and Evaluating Clinical Reasoning Trajectories for Medical Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05090)
*Yunqi Zhu,Wensheng Zhang,Xuebing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedTraj框架，将医学AI智能体的推理轨迹作为构建、评估和优化的核心对象。该框架生成结构化多步推理链，从五个质量维度（连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性、可追溯性）评分，通过受控错误注入建立因果联系，基于边际贡献进行步级过滤，并通过质量加权上下文学习在推理时回馈评估结果。在CareQA、PubMedQA和CECMed数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学AI智能体评估主要采用以答案为中心的范式，仅评估最终输出的正确性，忽略了中间推理质量。但在临床场景中，即使最终答案正确，若基于捏造证据或逻辑不连贯，其危害与错误答案无异，因此需要将推理轨迹本身作为评估和优化的核心对象。

Method: 提出MedTraj流水线：(1)从医学推理源生成结构化多步推理链；(2)将轨迹解析为临床观察、证据、编号推理步骤和最终结论，并从连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性、可追溯性五个维度评分；(3)通过受控错误注入建立特定推理失败与质量退化间的因果联系；(4)基于边际贡献的步级过滤识别驱动或削弱轨迹质量的关键推理步骤；(5)质量加权上下文学习将轨迹评估回馈到模型推理阶段，使其从强弱推理示范中学习。

Result: 在CareQA、PubMedQA、CECMed上，轨迹上下文一致提升推理连贯性，相比zero-shot基线增益为+0.029至+0.041。在CECMed上，质量加权上下文使正确率相对zero-shot基线近乎翻倍，同时幻觉率降低87%。边际贡献分析表明少数推理步骤承载了大部分质量信号，且推理链超过四步后收益递减。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹质量对于医学AI至关重要，MedTraj通过将轨迹作为构建、评估与优化的核心对象，能够有效提升推理连贯性、显著降低幻觉并提升答案正确性。该框架验证了轨迹级质量评估和反馈在医学推理中的价值。

Abstract: Evaluation of medical artificial intelligence agents remains predominantly answer-centric, assessing only the correctness of final outputs while overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning.
